## review-20260919T185037Z-6da2aa27

Durable findings from this review, for prior-work.md.

1. CORRECTION TO A STRUCTURAL CLAIM REPEATED IN TWO EARLIER REVIEWS, AND THE EVIDENCE THAT REPLACES IT. Realized triplets are NOT structurally equal to count = min(budget, len(groups)). select_triplets builds a cost matrix with `count` extra `missing_cost` columns and takes linear_sum_assignment, so a target configuration that violates Hall's condition over the geometry groups yields fewer selected triplets than `count`; `shortfall = budget - len(selected)` is recorded precisely because this can happen. The earlier reviews' "count-preserving by construction" argument was therefore an overstatement, even though its conclusion held. The correct and stronger evidence is empirical and was read from the sealed baseline accounting (baseline/triplet_accounting.json): the historical control produced EXACTLY 93,846 triplets in every one of its ten training epochs (sum 938,460, visits 35,030), despite drawing independent random targets each epoch. Realized counts are thus empirically invariant to target placement across the incumbent's own draw distribution. Any candidate whose per-epoch target law is contained in, or equal to, the incumbent's inherits that invariance; a candidate that moves targets OUTSIDE the incumbent's support (e.g. a width or shape change) does not, and needs the replay to establish it. Future candidates should cite this per-epoch identity rather than the retired structural argument.

2. THE SEALED BASELINE'S VALIDATION SELECTION IDENTITY IS 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059, single across its validation rows at 24,188 triplets, and its per-epoch training hashes are all distinct (epoch 0 c5043f46..., epoch 1 d81162e3..., etc.). This value was not previously recorded in campaign state and is the reference any candidate claiming an unchanged deterministic validation path must reproduce bit-for-bit. Every candidate so far has changed it; run-level-stratified-jitter is the first predicted to preserve it, which converts a soft expectation into a hard falsifiable launch check.

3. A PER-EPOCH JOINT-LAW IDENTITY IS ACHIEVABLE AND IS A STRONGER PROPERTY THAN A MARGINAL IDENTITY. The stratified construction `percentiles = (arange(count) + (strata + u)/E)/count` with `strata` the phase-th element of an independently permuted row per band was verified numerically to (a) keep every target inside its incumbent band, (b) give each band a uniform within-band position (KS p 0.111-0.996 over 20,000 draws), and (c) leave bands mutually independent within an epoch (max |off-diagonal correlation| 0.0098). Together these mean the per-epoch JOINT distribution over all bands equals the incumbent's law exactly, not merely each marginal. Any future cross-epoch or coupling mechanism should be held to this standard: demonstrate the per-epoch joint, not just the marginal, because count preservation and exploitation arguments both rest on the joint.

4. THE INCUMBENT-DEGENERATION IDIOM IS NOW CONFIRMED THREE TIMES AND SHOULD BE TREATED AS A REQUIREMENT. The coronal split-normal degenerated to the incumbent when the two side widths were equal; cross-slot parity interleaving degenerated when band_role was None; this candidate degenerates bit-for-bit when stratification_depth = 1, verified with np.allclose against the real incumbent expression. Every per-slot, per-side, per-plane or per-epoch policy should expose a parameter value at which it provably collapses onto the incumbent, and that identity should be checked by execution rather than argued.

5. RNG STREAM DISCIPLINE FOR EPOCH-INDEPENDENT STATE. operation_seed(seed, study, kind, operation, epoch) hashes the operation string into its JSON payload, so a new operation name yields a genuinely independent stream. The pattern for run-level (epoch-independent) randomness is to pass a derived index in the epoch slot - here `cycle = epoch // E`, constant at 0 for a ten-epoch run at E = 10 - under a distinct operation name, leaving all per-epoch streams untouched. This keeps a genuine resume reproducible (same seed, same cycle, same permutation) without adding persistent state, and is the reusable recipe for any future cross-epoch mechanism.

6. ADDED-COST DATA POINT, CONFIRMING THE VECTORIZED-PATH PREFERENCE HOLDS FOR NON-PPF WORK TOO. One extra default_rng construction plus one np.random.Generator.permuted over a (count, E) ~ (6, 10) array benchmarks at 9.84 microseconds per select_triplets call, i.e. about 2.1 s of CPU over the roughly 210k calls in a full schedule (35,030 visits x ~6 slots). Even at the validated worst case that sampler CPU converts to wall clock roughly 1:1 rather than being amortized across the 16 dataloader workers, that is negligible against the ~233 s headroom. Contrast with the coronal split-normal's +24.6 s bought by ~290k scalar truncnorm.ppf calls. The standing rule stands: express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call; per-element Python loops need explicit budget justification.

7. GATE FACTS RECONFIRMED. gates.py allowlists configuration keys under the `data.triplet_sampling.` prefix (and the bare `data.triplet_sampling` node) in addition to LOGISTICAL_KEYS and SAMPLING_KEYS, so that namespace is the correct home for any new sampler parameter; trainer.* remains off limits. accounting.py derives selection_sha256 from the sorted (series_type_index, selected paths) rows and never reads selection.metadata, so new diagnostic metadata keys are free to add. TripletLedger.observe counts from batch["slice_mask"].sum(dim=1) on real batches, so the zero-tolerance gate measures real selections with padding excluded.

8. Standing items carried forward unchanged: sampler changes must live in modules listed in identity.PREPROCESSING_SOURCES (data/preprocessing/*.py and data/dataset.py in; data/triplet_sampling/ out); any SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit makes preprocessing_identity differ, so such comparisons need --allowed-input-identity-differences preprocessing_identity as a standing expectation - note this is compatible with an UNCHANGED validation selection hash, and the two must not be conflated; realized depth histograms from the CPU replay, never target distributions, are the evidence for shape- or coverage-motivated candidates; all cache-derived motivating statistics in this campaign come from the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort and have never been re-measured on the training cohort; the draw-shape family is closed by three independent nulls; both imported ideas are owned by this campaign and docs/backlog/untried-ideas.md entries are read-only provenance; the old 0.003/0.008 single-run heuristics remain superseded by the sealed paired-bootstrap rule; and the comparison tool's limitations (one training seed, one development fold, intervals excluding training-seed and checkpoint-selection variability, allowed config and identity differences moving together) remain the honest frame for every decision here.

Review event: APPROVE run-level-stratified-jitter (experiment run-level-stratified-jitter-20260919T183826Z-f211c7ce) and keep it as the reserved head. No proposals in this event, nothing parked. Live queue depth is 3, BELOW the refill threshold of 4, so a researcher refill toward 5 IS requested now and may run concurrently with the head's detached training.

APPROVAL BASIS (independent verification, not the author's assertion).

1. Scope. `diff -rq` against parent_source (baseline/source) shows exactly four differing files: configs/training.yaml, src/knee_abnormality/config.py, src/knee_abnormality/data/dataset.py, src/knee_abnormality/data/preprocessing/sampling.py. These match the allowed EDIT_FILES set. `diff -rq source workspace` is empty apart from a .ruff_cache directory left in the workspace only (not in the immutable snapshot), so the snapshot is complete. The pre-existing empty src/knee_abnormality/data/triplet_sampling/ scaffold contains no files and is not imported; dataset.py and sampling.py are both in identity.PREPROCESSING_SOURCES, so the sampler logic keeps provenance fidelity.

2. Mechanism verified BY EXECUTION against the candidate's own operation_seed, not by inspection. The change is `cycle, phase = divmod(epoch, E)`; `strata = rng("strata", cycle).permuted(tile(arange(E), (count,1)), axis=1)[:, phase]`; `percentiles = (arange(count) + (strata + rng("targets").random(count))/E)/count`. Verified: (a) every produced percentile satisfies i/count <= p_i < (i+1)/count for all ten epochs, so each target stays inside its incumbent band and the incumbent band partition is preserved EXACTLY; (b) each band's realized stratum sequence over ten epochs is exactly a permutation of 0..9, i.e. genuine run-level stratification without replacement; (c) with stratification_depth=1 the expression reproduces the incumbent `(arange(count)+rng("targets").random(count))/count` bit-for-bit (np.allclose over multiple epochs), the incumbent-degeneration identity this campaign now requires of every per-slot/per-side policy; (d) over 20,000 synthetic studies at a fixed epoch, each band's within-band position is KS-indistinguishable from Uniform(0,1) (p = 0.111, 0.996, 0.835, 0.875, 0.530) and the maximum absolute off-diagonal correlation between bands is 0.0098. Point (d) is stronger than the developer's claim and is the key result: the per-epoch JOINT distribution over all bands is exactly the incumbent's law (independent uniforms within each band), not merely the per-band marginal. Only the coupling between epochs changes, which is precisely the idea's mechanism.

3. COUNT PRESERVATION, now supported by direct evidence rather than only by structure. Contrary to the assumption used in earlier reviews, realized triplets are NOT structurally min(budget, len(groups)): select_triplets fills a linear_sum_assignment with `missing_cost` columns, so a target configuration could in principle shortfall. I resolved this empirically from the sealed baseline accounting (baseline/triplet_accounting.json): the control produced exactly 93,846 triplets in EVERY one of its ten training epochs (sum 938,460, visits 35,030) even though each epoch drew independent random targets. Realized counts are therefore invariant to target placement across the incumbent's own draw distribution. Since this candidate's per-epoch target law is exactly that same distribution (point 2d), its per-epoch counts are drawn from the identical, empirically degenerate, distribution. This retires the developer's honestly flagged shortfall concern; the launch-time replay remains the confirmation, not a worry.

4. Validation independence and identity. The non-training branch is now written explicitly as `(arange(count)+0.5)/count`, byte-identical in value to the incumbent expression with training=False; the stratification code is unreachable when training_seed is None, and strata derive from training_seed anyway. Deterministic validation is therefore unchanged and seed-independent. Crucially, this candidate is the first in the campaign expected to REPRODUCE the incumbent validation selection identity exactly, and the reference value is available: the sealed baseline accounting records a single validation selection_sha256 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 across its rows at 24,188 triplets. That exact hash is a sharp, falsifiable launch check, which earlier reviews asserted without a reference value. Training selection remains stochastic: the strata stream is keyed by cycle (0 throughout a ten-epoch run at E=10) but the "targets" jitter stream still varies by epoch and the stratum index varies by phase, so per-epoch training hashes must still differ and changed_training_studies must stay non-empty.

5. RNG hygiene. `def rng(operation, epoch=epoch)` shadows the closure epoch with a default argument so `rng("strata", cycle)` is epoch-independent within a cycle while all other streams keep their per-epoch keying. operation_seed hashes the operation string into the payload, so "strata" is a genuinely distinct stream from "targets", "suppliers" and "band_order"; no stream is reused or aliased. With E=10 and max_epochs=10, cycle is always 0, so the whole run uses one permutation per band and wrap-around never triggers; a resumed run retaining its seed reproduces the same permutation, satisfying the idea's reproducibility requirement.

6. Config gate and threading. The new key is data.triplet_sampling.stratification_depth on a new TripletSamplingConfig (StrictInt, gt=0, default 10), which gates.py explicitly allowlists via its `data.triplet_sampling.` prefix branch. The config resolves through ExperimentConfig with depth 10, max_epochs 10, sigma fraction unchanged at 0.867. E is correctly taken from config and NOT from trainer.max_epochs, as required. dataset.py passes self.data.triplet_sampling.stratification_depth positionally at the existing call site; self.data is the DataConfig, so the attribute path is valid. dataset.py is the only production caller; the two adhoc scripts fall back to the default and are outside the run path.

7. Leakage, coverage, exploitation. No label, fold, cohort, grouping, scoring or checkpoint-selection code is touched. No validation-derived feedback, no persistent precomputation, no extra decode pass. Per-epoch band masses and the truncation support are untouched, so exploitation is identical epoch by epoch; the coverage gain is bought purely from removing duplicate sub-band visits across the run. The new metadata key "stratification_depth" cannot perturb identity: accounting.py computes selection_sha256 from the sorted (series_type_index, selected paths) rows and never reads selection.metadata.

8. Added cost measured, not assumed. The added work is one default_rng construction plus one np.random.Generator.permuted over a (count, E) array per select_triplets call. Benchmarked on this machine at 9.84 microseconds per call; at roughly 210k calls per run (35,030 visits x ~6 slots) that is about 2.1 s of CPU across the whole schedule. Even under the campaign's validated worst case that sampler CPU converts to wall clock roughly 1:1 rather than being amortized by dataloader workers, this is negligible against the 233 s of headroom between the 1,551.5 s control and the 1,785 s ceiling. The vectorized truncnorm.ppf call is untouched, satisfying the standing design requirement; no per-element Python loop is introduced.

NON-BLOCKING OBSERVATIONS (neither justifies the one allowed repair):
(a) The permutation array is shaped (count, E), so if a study/kind's realized `count` ever varied between epochs the permutation would change shape and the run-level stratification would degrade to a per-cycle re-draw. count derives from deterministic pool geometry and budget, and the baseline's identical per-epoch totals indicate it is stable, so this is a graceful-degradation path rather than a defect.
(b) SAMPLING_IDENTITY["targets"] was updated to "run_level_stratified_...", which is correct bookkeeping and makes preprocessing_identity differ. Note the distinction for this candidate: preprocessing_identity differs while the validation SELECTION hash must still match the baseline exactly.
(c) The default-argument shadowing in `rng` is terse but correct and matches the repository's comment-free idiom.

LAUNCH CONDITIONS the supervisor must honor (no limit relaxed, none inherited from any winner):
1. One-time full-schedule accounting.calibrate() CPU replay against this candidate source BEFORE any GPU run, confirming training_triplets exactly 938460, training_visits exactly 35030, validation_triplets exactly 24188 per pass at zero tolerance.
2. In the same replay, confirm the deterministic validation selection_sha256 equals the sealed baseline value 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 exactly and is identical across all ten validation rows. This candidate is unique in the campaign so far in predicting an UNCHANGED validation identity; any deviation is a hard failure of the stated mechanism, not a recordable new identity.
3. In the same replay, read back REALIZED middle depths per (study, kind) across the ten epochs and re-derive the motivating coverage statistic - distinct realized middles visited per pool over the run - against the incumbent. The +14.7% (24.29 -> 27.87) figure is a nearest-depth approximation on the fold-0 VALIDATION-cohort token cache and is not evidence of realized exposure, since geometry_groups plus linear_sum_assignment snap targets onto existing discrete candidate slices. Also confirm the ten per-epoch training selection_sha256 values are all distinct (stochasticity preserved).
4. Comparison requires --allowed-input-identity-differences preprocessing_identity, since SAMPLING_IDENTITY was intentionally updated and both changed .py files are in PREPROCESSING_SOURCES. Allowed config differences must reduce to the sealed comparison_keys (name, paths.output_root, seed) plus the allowlisted data.triplet_sampling.stratification_depth.
5. Draw and record a fresh seed at launch, replacing the pre-existing 2583306572 still present in the candidate's configs/training.yaml.
6. Reference incumbent is historical-control, run overnight-20260919T120130Z-i2oqlnls/runs/00-control, predictions sha256 6f8f0ef5..., macro AUC 0.894037, under the sealed 2,000-draw paired report-group bootstrap at resampling seed 20260914, 95% interval.

RANKING of the live queue (3 entries; head reserved and first by the reservation rule).
1. run-level-stratified-jitter - RESERVED HEAD, approved here, status prepared. The only queued idea on the campaign's named cross-training-evolution axis, and now the cleanest contract profile any candidate has had: per-epoch joint law exactly the incumbent's, band partition preserved, validation identity predicted to hold bit-for-bit against a known reference hash, ~2 s added cost. Its expected magnitude is modest because it changes the sequence of bags rather than the content of any one bag, but after three nulls in the draw-shape family it is the strongest remaining test of whether cross-run coverage - as opposed to per-epoch density - matters at all. Both outcomes are informative and the null is cheap to interpret because the marginal is held fixed by construction.
2. entitlement-balanced-supplier-draw - unchanged at second. Real, source-verified train/validation supplier-policy mismatch (training `choices[supplier_rng.integers(len(choices))]` versus validation `max(choices, key=entitlement - assigned)`, with entitlement already accumulated as exact Fractions on both paths and discarded in training), confined to one loop, and it changes SOURCE selection rather than reshaping a density over an unchanged candidate set - the direction the campaign's evidence now favours. Ranked second only on reach: 15.1% of pools (660 of 4,368) can move at all. Balance alone, no quality weighting, so the arm isolates one factor; rng("band_order") is the natural carrier for a randomized start.
3. physical-span-standardized-triplets - unchanged at last. The most original mechanism and the only one that changes what a triplet IS, but the narrowest reach (~6% of pools below 1 mm median spacing) and the only one needing a scope widening. The grant of data/preprocessing/preparation.py, limited to the consecutive-slice invariant (preparation.py:125-130) and its immediate callers, stands for when it reaches development, together with its two preconditions: inspect the p1 0.03 mm pools before assuming they are recoverable, and confirm unique-decode counts in the replay since decoding is the runtime-sensitive part.

REFILL. Depth is 3, below the threshold of 4, so a researcher refill toward 5 is requested now and should run concurrently with the head's detached training; at most one refill in flight. The refill must carry forward the retained surplus (bimodal / compartment-weighted sagittal draw, which survives the shape nulls on its own terms since its claim is that no unimodal density can express the measured sagittal flank preference, but which should still rank below any content- or coverage-changing alternative). It must deduplicate against mechanism_ids cross-epoch-stratification, training-supplier-allocation, through-plane-triplet-composition, within-plane-depth-decorrelation, coronal-side-relative-distribution and plane-specific-sampling-shape, and must not requeue the closed directions: geometry-informed-plane-widths (including the exact mean-preserving 46.65/34.55/38.70 mm ratio variant), coronal-side-relative-draw (including any posterior-favouring re-normalization), per-type centre offsets, common-factor sigma sweeps, fixed-total reallocations, repeated_series_share, side-conditioned sagittal sampling, intra-pool overlap/repulsion, and capacity-recovery / shortfall reallocation. Instruct it to prioritise mechanisms that change bag CONTENT, cross-epoch coverage, source selection, or what a triplet is, and to treat further reshaping of the target density over an unchanged candidate set as low value unless it carries new realized-exposure evidence. The at-most-one-in-flight rule stands: no idea below the head enters development until the head's launch removes it.

## proposals-20260919T190312Z-3039dad1

Durable findings from this proposals event, for prior-work.md.

1. CAMPAIGN-LEVEL DECOMPOSITION - THE SINGLE MOST USEFUL RESULT RECORDED SO FAR, AND IT EXPLAINS THREE NULLS. On the fold-0 token cache (outputs/analysis/t/token-cache-20260917, 161,466 candidates, three retired sigma-40 ranked-filtered checkpoints, training-scale counts 6/6/5/5/5/5, procedure validated by reproducing the documented deterministic macro AUC 0.88727 to five decimals), frozen-model bag quality at MATCHED counts decomposes as: stratified truncated Gaussian 0.88746, equal-spaced stratified 0.88559, i.i.d. truncated Gaussian with the same marginal but no stratification 0.87823, uniform random over candidates 0.87540, uniform random over geometry groups 0.87423, most-central groups 0.87414. Reading: REGULARITY of the realized depth set is worth about +0.009 to +0.013; the SHAPE of the density over an unchanged candidate set is worth at most about +0.002. This is a quantitative explanation for the three closed shape nulls (common-factor sigma sweeps, coronal side-relative widths, per-plane widths) and it redirects the campaign: rank regularity, coupling, coverage, content, source and triplet-definition mechanisms above any further density reshaping. Standing caveat: this is frozen-model bag quality on the VALIDATION cohort, not a training effect, and the same evidence class has failed three times to predict a training outcome.

2. THE INCUMBENT TRAINING DRAW IS MEASURABLY WORSE THAN ITS OWN DETERMINISTIC COUNTERPART, AT IDENTICAL COUNTS AND IDENTICAL MARGINALS. Independent per-band jitter makes adjacent bands collide: minimum gap between selected middles has p10 3.38 mm and median 7.97 mm under the training draw, against 9.92 mm and 13.71 mm for the deterministic bag, and frozen-model macro AUC 0.88494 (sd 0.00060) against 0.88730. So the training draw sits about 0.0023 below the deterministic bag purely through within-bag clumping. Halving the jitter amplitude recovers about half of that gap (0.88612); a shared per-bag offset (Cranley-Patterson rotation of the equal-mass lattice) recovers about 91% of it (0.88703) at full amplitude with coverage measured unchanged (23.63 vs 23.73 distinct geometry groups per pool over ten epochs). Record the annealing variant as measured-and-dominated so it is not proposed again.

3. COUNT-PRESERVATION ARGUMENTS ARE CONDITIONAL ON THE JOINT LAW, NOT ONLY THE MARGINAL - AN IMPORTANT REFINEMENT OF THE REVISION-61 FINDING. The empirical invariance (the control produced exactly 93,846 triplets in every one of its ten training epochs despite independent random targets) establishes that realized counts are invariant to target placement ACROSS THE INCUMBENT'S OWN DRAW DISTRIBUTION. A candidate whose per-epoch joint law equals the incumbent's (e.g. run-level-stratified-jitter) inherits that invariance. A candidate that changes the joint law - a shared-offset rotation, a width change, a shape change - does not, and must establish count preservation in the replay. `count = min(budget, len(groups))` (sampling.py:451) bounds the request, but linear_sum_assignment with `missing_cost` columns (sampling.py:526-535) can still shortfall. Corollary worth stating: regularizing mechanisms should if anything REDUCE shortfall risk, since more separated targets are easier to satisfy over discrete geometry groups.

4. FOUR HYPOTHESES FALSIFIED BY MEASUREMENT THIS SESSION; RECORD THEM SO THEY ARE NOT RE-PROPOSED. (a) LEARNED PER-TRIPLET FEEDBACK IS NOT USABLE AT THIS BAG SIZE. Attention is nearly uniform over a pool (effective candidates / pool size median 0.847/0.865/0.869 across three checkpoints); per-study utility centroids have sd only 6.2 mm and correlate 0.709 with the pool's plain mean candidate depth; cross-model transfer fails (band-wise top-contribution selection scored by seed42 gives +0.0025 on seed43 and -0.0013 on seed44) and dropping stratification for globally top-scoring candidates collapses to 0.88127 against 0.88731. The contract permits training-only sampling feedback, so this closes a permitted-but-empty direction on evidence rather than on scope. (b) THE SUPPLIER-PREFERENCE COST IS NOT A WEAKNESS. `change_cost = count + 1` dominates the normalized placement error, which looked like a way for multi-source pools to lose regularity; measured the opposite - placement error is 1.14 mm median (p90 3.78) in multi-source pools against 1.26 mm (p90 4.20) in single-source pools, and a supplier-blind nearest-group selection does not beat the pipeline on the 436 multi-source-slot studies (+0.0010, +0.0005, -0.0012). (c) NO NEW STRATIFICATION AXIS IS AVAILABLE. Within-pool variance of per-triplet contribution explained by a cubic in depth is median 0.468; by source series 0.051 (and only in 660 of 4,368 pools); by run, not measurable because 0 of 4,368 fold-0 pools contain more than one run - which also retires any run-splitting or run-diversity idea on this fold. Depth is the only informative observable at selection time and the sampler already stratifies it. (d) THE DEPTH ORIGIN IS PRESCRIPTION GEOMETRY, DELIBERATELY: `fit_center` (geometry.py:183-217) solves for the least-squares intersection of each series' in-plane image-centre line using only the projection I - nn^T, discarding each series' own slab midpoint along its normal (which filtering.py already computes as `volume_center` for the other-knee split). The residual per-study anatomical misalignment this could correct is about 4 mm against a utility field documented flat within +/-30 mm.

5. DROPOUT-AWARE BUDGET REDIRECTION IS CONTRACT-BLOCKED, NOT MERELY UNPROMISING. Series dropout runs inside the model after the dataset has decoded everything (`_effective_presence`, model.py), so a share of each visit's decoded triplets is discarded; redirecting that budget to surviving slots would raise effective exposure at unchanged accounted counts, but it requires moving the dropout draw into the dataset and setting model.series_dropout to zero, and the gate restricts config differences to data.triplet_sampling.* plus the logistical keys. Record as blocked so it is not re-derived.

6. PER-EPOCH BAG SIZE IS AN UNUSED DEGREE OF FREEDOM INSIDE THE SEALED LIMITS. accounting.py:96-99 and finish() sum `triplets` and `visits` over the per-epoch rows, and gates.py:101-105 compares those SUMS to 938,460 and 35,030, while only validation rows are checked individually at 24,188. Per-epoch training bag size is therefore unconstrained as long as the run total is exact. Combined with the closed allocation investigation's asymmetry (training-time counts matter; test-time counts barely move a frozen model) and the steeply concave frozen-model count curve (mean triplets 14.5 -> 0.87842, 25.4 -> 0.88727, 39.9 -> 0.89057, 50.7 -> 0.89073), a +/-1 per-slot offset moves a bag between about 20 and 31 triplets, on the steep part of that curve. Honest counterweight to record alongside it: by Jensen, a size-paced schedule has slightly LOWER average per-epoch bag quality than a constant schedule at the same mean, so any claim here is a training-dynamics claim, not a bag-quality claim.

7. DRAW-DEPENDENT INTENSITY WINDOW - A REAL COUPLING, FLAGGED NOT PURSUED. `prepare_series` sets each series' intensity window over the unique slices the draw selected, so the same slice is normalized differently depending on the epoch's draw; the cache README quantifies the window shift at a median 1.6% of span (p90 5.3%, p99 12.9%, max 46%) with pooled-logit effects of mean 0.019-0.022. Making the window draw-invariant is an intensity-normalization change requiring extra decodes and reads as the surrounding recipe rather than triplet selection, so it is out of campaign scope. Worth recording that a less clumped, more consistent draw partially mitigates it as a side effect.

8. NARROW-REACH FACTS FOR FUTURE SIZING. Non-physical (local, normalized-[0,1], uniform-draw fallback) pools are 150 of 4,368 across 31 of 904 studies (3.4%) - narrower than the last-ranked queued idea, so no local-pool mechanism should be queued on reach grounds alone.

9. Standing items carried forward unchanged: sampler changes must live in modules listed in identity.PREPROCESSING_SOURCES (data/preprocessing/*.py and data/dataset.py in; data/triplet_sampling/ out); any SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit makes preprocessing_identity differ, so such comparisons need --allowed-input-identity-differences preprocessing_identity, which is compatible with an UNCHANGED validation selection hash and must not be conflated with it; the sealed baseline validation selection_sha256 is 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059; realized depth histograms from the CPU replay, never target distributions, are the evidence for shape- or coverage-motivated candidates; every per-slot/per-side/per-epoch policy must expose a parameter value at which it provably degenerates to the incumbent, checked by execution; express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call, since a per-element Python ppf loop measured +24.6 s of wall clock nearly 1:1 with its CPU cost against only ~233 s of headroom; all cache-derived motivating statistics in this campaign come from the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort and have never been re-measured on the training cohort; both imported ideas are owned by this campaign and docs/backlog/untried-ideas.md entries are read-only provenance; the old 0.003/0.008 single-run heuristics remain superseded by the sealed paired-bootstrap rule; and the comparison tool's limitations (one training seed, one development fold, intervals excluding training-seed and checkpoint-selection variability, allowed config and identity differences moving together) remain the honest frame for every decision here.

Proposals event: accept both proposals. Live queue goes 3 -> 5, at target, so NO researcher refill is requested. Nothing parked. Reserved head unchanged: new evidence reranks only the unclaimed tail.

1. run-level-stratified-jitter - RESERVED HEAD, status prepared, experiment run-level-stratified-jitter-20260919T183826Z-f211c7ce, approved at revision 61. First by the reservation rule; nothing in this event displaces it. Its six launch conditions stand verbatim and unrelaxed: (1) one-time full-schedule accounting.calibrate() CPU replay before any GPU run confirming 938460 / 35030 / 24188 at zero tolerance; (2) in the same replay, deterministic validation selection_sha256 must equal the sealed baseline 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 exactly and be identical across all ten validation rows - any deviation is a hard failure of the stated mechanism, not a new recordable identity; (3) read back REALIZED middle depths and re-derive distinct-middles-per-pool over the run against the incumbent, and confirm the ten per-epoch training hashes are distinct; (4) comparison with --allowed-input-identity-differences preprocessing_identity, allowed config differences reducing to name / paths.output_root / seed plus data.triplet_sampling.stratification_depth; (5) fresh drawn seed replacing 2583306572; (6) reference incumbent historical-control under the sealed 2,000-draw paired report-group bootstrap at seed 20260914.

NOTE ON THE NEW HEAD-ADJACENT EVIDENCE. The researcher's decomposition (regularity of the realized set worth ~+0.009-0.013 at matched counts; density shape worth at most ~+0.002) arrives after the head was prepared. It does not weaken the head - the head holds the per-epoch joint law fixed and changes only cross-epoch coupling, so it remains the clean test of whether cross-run coverage matters - but it does mean the head and the new second entry both live in the newly identified high-value family (regularity/coupling of the realized set) rather than the now thrice-null density-shape family. That is a reason to run both, in this order, not to reorder them.

2. common-offset-lattice-jitter (NEW, accepted, ranked 2 and by a clear margin over the rest of the tail). Verified against the sealed source: the training draw is exactly `percentiles = (arange(count) + rng("targets").random(count)) / count` (sampling.py:476-478), `count = min(budget, len(groups))` (sampling.py:451), and the validation branch is the literal 0.5 - so a Cranley-Patterson rotation `(arange(count) + u)/count` with one scalar u is a one-expression change that leaves the validation path untouched and each band's marginal exactly uniform on its own interval. It is the only proposal in this refill with a directly measured effect (frozen-model bag quality 0.88494 -> 0.88703 against the deterministic bag's 0.88730, per-rep sd 0.00060/0.00084 over eight repetitions and three checkpoints), it is strictly cheaper than the incumbent (one RNG draw instead of `count`, vectorized truncnorm.ppf untouched, so the standing per-element-loop budget rule is satisfied), measured coverage is unchanged (23.63 vs 23.73 distinct geometry groups per pool over ten epochs), and it does not duplicate any closed direction or any in-flight mechanism.

Three qualifications carried into its assignment, none of which justify demoting it:
(a) The evidence class is frozen-model bag quality on the validation cohort under retired sigma-40 checkpoints, which is the same class of evidence that failed to predict the training effect three times in the shape family. The measurement here is of a different quantity (regularity at matched counts, not density), which is why it still ranks second, but the result must be read as a prediction, not an outcome.
(b) COUNT PRESERVATION IS NOT INHERITED FROM THE HEAD'S ARGUMENT. The empirical count-invariance recorded at revision 61 (the control produced exactly 93,846 triplets in every one of its ten epochs) covers the incumbent's joint law. This candidate deliberately changes that joint law: the bag becomes a rigid rotated lattice rather than independent per-band draws. More regular targets should if anything make the linear_sum_assignment easier to satisfy, so shortfall risk falls, but that must be established by the replay rather than assumed, and the assignment should say so explicitly.
(c) The candidate reduces the entropy of the per-epoch draw from `count` scalars to one. The honest counter-hypothesis the researcher states - that within-bag clumping may act as a useful regularizer, and that the rotation trades within-bag discrepancy for a coherently wobbling bag centroid across epochs - is real and makes both outcomes informative.

Fixed constraint for its development, adopting the researcher's recommendation: the shared offset is drawn per (study, kind, epoch) and shared across THAT SLOT's bands only. It must NOT be shared across a plane's two slots while cross-slot-band-interleaving is in flight, because the two mechanisms would collide and the arm would stop isolating one factor. No residual per-band jitter variant, no second arm. The validation branch must be left literally untouched, so the sealed baseline validation selection_sha256 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 is the expected launch check, exactly as for the head. Verification must read back REALIZED middle depths, not targets, and report the min-gap statistic (incumbent p10 about 3.4 mm, median about 8.0 mm) and distinct realized middles over ten epochs. No new config key is expected; if one is added it must sit under data.triplet_sampling.*.

3. epoch-paced-bag-size (NEW, accepted, ranked 3). It is the only proposal that uses a contract degree of freedom nobody has used - per-epoch bag size at an unchanged run total - and it is independent of the head and of entry 2, so it cannot be made redundant by either. Verified premise: the gate compares SUMS of triplets and visits to the sealed 938,460 / 35,030 while checking validation rows individually at 24,188, so a zero-sum per-epoch offset is inside the sealed limits. Ranked below entry 2 because its expected sign is argued rather than measured, it carries the researcher's own honestly stated Jensen strike (the frozen-model count curve is concave, so a paced schedule has slightly lower average per-epoch bag quality at the same mean, making this strictly a training-dynamics claim), and it is the only one of the two with open memory and runtime questions.

Constraints for its eventual assignment: the offsets must sum to zero over the run and the eligibility rule must be epoch-invariant (it keys on `len(groups)`, which is deterministic per slot), so every eligible slot realizes exactly 10*budget and every ineligible slot is untouched - exact count neutrality by construction, not by tuning. It must be gated on training_seed is not None so all ten validation rows stay at 24,188 and the baseline validation selection hash is reproduced. Two things the developer must MEASURE rather than assume, and report before any launch request: peak activation memory in the large-bag epochs (roughly +19% of tokens per study at +1 on every slot - if any fixed token/padding capacity is exceeded the counts themselves are at risk, which would be a contract failure, not a performance note), and total wall clock against the 1,785 s ceiling in the replay, since decode cost is not exactly linear in triplet count. If either binds, reduce the offset magnitude or the set of slots carrying it rather than relaxing anything. One direction only (smaller early, larger late); the reverse direction must not be spawned as arm two.

4. entitlement-balanced-supplier-draw - unchanged at fourth (was second). Demoted purely by the arrival of two stronger entries, not by new negative evidence. The train/validation supplier-policy mismatch is still source-verified at sampling.py:494-498 (training `choices[int(supplier_rng.integers(len(choices)))]` versus validation `max(choices, key=entitlement - assigned)`, with entitlement accumulated as exact Fractions on both paths at sampling.py:492-493 and discarded in training), it is confined to one loop, and it changes SOURCE selection rather than reshaping a density. Reach remains the limiter: 15.1% of pools (660 of 4,368) can move at all. The researcher's item 4 this session further bounds it - source series explain a median 0.051 of within-pool contribution variance, and only in the 660 multi-series pools - so it should be read as a small-reach, matched-condition-alignment arm. Balance alone, no quality weighting; rng("band_order") carries the randomized start.

5. physical-span-standardized-triplets - unchanged at last. Most original mechanism and the only one that changes what a triplet IS, but narrowest reach (~6% of pools below 1 mm median spacing) and the only one needing a scope widening. The grant of data/preprocessing/preparation.py, limited to the consecutive-slice invariant (preparation.py:125-130) and its immediate callers, stands for when it reaches development, with its two preconditions unchanged: inspect the p1 0.03 mm pools before assuming they are recoverable, and confirm unique-decode counts in the replay since decoding is the runtime-sensitive part.

SURPLUS RETAINED, NOT QUEUED: the bimodal / compartment-weighted sagittal draw. It survives on its own terms (no unimodal density can express the measured sagittal flank preference) but the new decomposition bounds its whole family at about +0.002, so it stays unqueued unless it acquires realized-exposure evidence. I am not queueing it merely to fill depth.

CLOSED DIRECTIONS, now extended by this session's falsifications and not to be requeued: learned per-triplet model-attention feedback (falsified - attention near-uniform, effective/pool median 0.847-0.869; no cross-seed transfer, +0.0025 on seed43 and -0.0013 on seed44; unstratified top-scoring collapses to 0.88127); per-study anatomical anchor / re-estimated depth origin (too small - ~4 mm against a flat +/-30 mm utility band); supplier-preference-degrading-placement (hypothesis falsified in the opposite direction - multi-source placement error is LOWER, 1.14 vs 1.26 mm median); new stratification axes (depth explains median 0.468 of within-pool variance, source series 0.051, and 0 of 4,368 fold-0 pools contain more than one run, which also retires run-splitting/run-diversity on this fold); dropout-aware budget redirection (contract-blocked - requires setting model.series_dropout to zero, outside the allowed key surface); non-physical/local pools (3.4% of pools, narrower than the last-ranked entry); jitter-amplitude annealing (measured 0.88612, dominated by entry 2's 0.88703 at full amplitude); between-study budget reallocation (inside the closed fixed-total family and not exactly count-neutral). ## completion-20260919T190547Z-338cfefe

Durable findings from the cross-slot-band-interleaving completion, for prior-work.md.

1. RESULT OF RECORD. cross-slot-band-interleaving (for a plane whose two contrast slots both have raw sources, each slot draws its n-band percentile lattice from alternating sub-bands of a shared 2n-band grid; role fixed by config order, role 0 = fluid_sensitive 0) is INCONCLUSIVE; the incumbent is retained. Reference historical-control macro AUC 0.8940374, candidate 0.8923722, delta -0.0016652; paired report-group bootstrap delta mean -0.0015824, 95% interval [-0.0058750, +0.0027358], 852 groups, 2000/2000 valid draws, resampling seed 20260914, training seed 769982658, candidate selected epoch 7 vs reference epoch 9, total runtime 1581.57 s of the 1785 s ceiling. Evidence: experiments/cross-slot-band-interleaving-20260919T181018Z-c008c6bb/comparison/comparison.json. Candidate deterministic validation identity: selection_sha256 430822ce2de8b8f1d4d8cd65ffe4ec95c129584933d0e7f20f1c12849cb2c47a, single and stable across all ten validation rows.

2. THE CAMPAIGN'S LARGEST MEASURED WEAKNESS DID NOT CONVERT, AND THE COUNTER-HYPOTHESIS IS NOW THE BETTER-SUPPORTED READING. Cross-slot depth duplication was the biggest anomaly the campaign had found (0.913 axial / 0.951 coronal / 0.968 sagittal of one slot's deterministic middles within 5 mm of a partner-slot middle, median nearest distance 0.00 mm, with 0 of 1,656 plane instances sharing a series). Removing it by parity interleaving produced a slightly negative point estimate. The honest reading recorded at approval — that co-located T1/PD and fluid triplets may be COMPLEMENTARY rather than redundant, i.e. that a registered cross-contrast pair at the same depth is information the model uses — is now the reading the data favour, though it is not established by an interval strictly below zero. Practical consequence for ranking: treat cross-slot / partner-slot decorrelation as a closed family, and stop treating "distinct anatomy in the bag" as automatically more valuable than "matched anatomy across contrasts". This is the campaign's first test of a bag-CONTENT mechanism (as opposed to density shape), and it did not beat the incumbent.

3. THE CONTENT-VERSUS-SHAPE RANKING HEURISTIC NEEDS SOFTENING, NOT REVERSAL. After the completion of geometry-informed-plane-widths the campaign adopted "prefer content / coverage / source / triplet-definition mechanisms over density reshaping". Four full-budget arms have now returned intervals straddling zero: common-factor sigma sweeps (flat), coronal side-relative widths (-0.0013), per-plane widths (+0.0004), cross-slot band interleaving (-0.0017). All four moved the fitted model substantially (mean absolute probability difference 0.0667, 0.0778, and 0.0863 here — the largest yet) while leaving fold-0 macro AUC inside +/-0.002. The correct current statement is narrower and more useful: no selection intervention tested so far, of either family, has moved fold-0 macro AUC beyond bootstrap noise, and the realistic per-arm effect size for this campaign is on the order of +/-0.002, against a 95% interval half-width of roughly 0.004. That ratio, not any family preference, is the dominant fact for future ranking: prefer mechanisms whose predicted effect exceeds ~0.004, or whose null is cheaply interpretable because a strong invariant is held fixed by construction.

4. THE MECHANISM CARRIED A CONFOUND THAT BOUNDS WHAT THE NULL RULES OUT. As recorded at review, parity interleaving does not only decorrelate the two slots: deterministically role 0 sits at (i+0.25)/n and role 1 at (i+0.75)/n against the incumbent (i+0.5)/n, so the fluid_sensitive=0 slot receives a systematically lower-percentile bag and the fluid_sensitive=1 slot a higher-percentile one, with only the plane AGGREGATE approximately preserved (exact only when both slots share support, sigma and count). The measured result is therefore for interleaving PLUS a contrast-conditioned depth shift. A future de-confounded variant is conceivable but must not be spawned as arm two of this idea; given finding 2 and finding 3 it should not be proposed at all without new realized-exposure evidence.

5. AN APPROVAL CONDITION WENT UNVERIFIED, AND THIS IS THE PROCESS LESSON. Approval condition 2 required the CPU replay to read back REALIZED middle depths and re-measure the 0.913/0.951/0.968 duplication statistic plus the per-study band_role distribution (expected both-slots-present shares ~20/75/89%). No artifact in the experiment directory records either. Counts and identities are confirmed from training/fold_0/triplet_accounting.json, so the contract holds, but there is no recorded confirmation that the intervention reduced realized cross-slot co-location as opposed to target co-location — and geometry_groups plus linear_sum_assignment snap targets onto each slot's own discrete candidate slices, which could absorb an arbitrary fraction of a 0.5/n percentile separation. The null is therefore of the implemented target-level intervention, not of realized de-duplication. Standing rule to enforce from here: a mechanism-level verification condition attached at approval must produce a recorded artifact, and a completion whose mechanism claim is unverified should say so explicitly in its finding rather than let the metric stand in for the mechanism.

6. PER-TARGET AND FAMILY DIAGNOSTICS (informative only; none individually resolvable at this sample size). Candidate minus reference: Lateral Meniscus +0.0067, Effusion +0.0040, Baker's +0.0032, Synovitis +0.0023, PF OA +0.0020, Medial Meniscus +0.0015, ACL +0.0008; Lateral OA -0.0028, Medial OA -0.0043, Fracture -0.0058, MCL -0.0109, Contusion -0.0168. Family bootstrap means: meniscus +0.0041 [-0.0054, +0.0137], oa -0.0036 [-0.0126, +0.0049], other_eight -0.0025 [-0.0078, +0.0026]. The one pattern worth recording is that the two largest losses are Contusion and MCL, both bone-marrow/soft-tissue findings whose reading depends on fluid-sensitive and non-fluid views of the SAME location; that is exactly what parity interleaving pulls apart, and it is the most mechanistically coherent trace of finding 2 in the per-target table. Post hoc from one run; must not justify a targeted retune. Note also oa_margin +0.0353 [-0.0030, +0.0748]: a wide margin variant, not signal, and consistent with the standing instruction that margin intervals are not to be read as evidence.

7. RUNTIME CONFIRMS THE INDEX-TRANSFORMATION DESIGN RULE A THIRD TIME. The change was expressed entirely as an integer index/denominator transformation upstream of an untouched vectorized truncnorm.ppf, and measured 1581.57 s against the 1551.5 s control (+30.0 s, the same order as the per-plane-width candidate's +29.7 s, i.e. schedule noise) versus the coronal split-normal's +24.6 s bought entirely by ~290k scalar ppf calls. Express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call; a per-element Python loop needs explicit budget justification against the ~233 s headroom.

8. COUNT INVARIANCE HELD FOR A JOINT-LAW-CHANGING CANDIDATE, WHICH IS A NEW DATA POINT. Unlike run-level-stratified-jitter, this candidate changed the per-epoch joint law (each slot's bands confined to alternating sub-bands of a 2n grid), so it could not inherit the revision-61 empirical invariance. It nonetheless produced exactly 93,846 triplets in every one of its ten epochs. ## review-20260919T191024Z-3f3ca1bb

Durable findings from this review, for prior-work.md.

1. A CANDIDATE CAN BE STRICTLY CHEAPER THAN THE INCUMBENT AND STILL CHANGE THE JOINT LAW. common-offset-lattice-jitter replaces `rng("targets").random(count)` with `rng("targets").random()`, so the per-epoch draw costs one scalar instead of `count` and the vectorized truncnorm.ppf is untouched. This is the first candidate in the campaign with negative added cost, and it shows the standing design rule (express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call) is not merely cost-neutral but can be cost-reducing. Verified by diff: exactly one file, two hunks, no config key, no new module.

2. THE INCUMBENT-DEGENERATION CHECK IS NOW CONFIRMED FIVE TIMES AND SHOULD BE TREATED AS A HARD REQUIREMENT. Equal side widths (coronal split-normal), band_role None (cross-slot parity), stratification_depth 1 (run-level strata), and now u = 0.5 for the shared bag rotation, which reproduces the deterministic validation lattice (arange(count)+0.5)/count bit-for-bit. Every per-slot, per-side, per-plane or per-epoch policy must expose a parameter value at which it provably collapses onto the incumbent, checked by execution.

3. BAND-PARTITION PRESERVATION IS A SEPARATE AND CHECKABLE PROPERTY FROM MARGINAL PRESERVATION, AND BOTH ARE SEPARATE FROM JOINT-LAW PRESERVATION. Verified numerically for this candidate: for any u in [0,1) and count in {1,5,6}, every percentile stays strictly inside its incumbent 1/count-wide band (so the support of each band is exactly unchanged), and band i's percentile (i+u)/count is exactly Uniform(i/count,(i+1)/count) (200,000-draw band-0 mean 0.083213 against the exact 1/12, range [1.1e-07, 0.1666661]). Yet the JOINT law is changed by construction: adjacent gaps become exactly 1/count, a rigid lattice, versus the incumbent's independent and often near-zero gaps. This three-level distinction (support / marginal / joint) is the right vocabulary for future candidates, because count-preservation arguments rest on the joint, coverage arguments on the marginal, and shape-null comparisons on the support.

4. RNG STREAM INDEPENDENCE MEANS DRAW-COUNT CHANGES ARE LOCAL. `rng(operation)` constructs a fresh default_rng from operation_seed(seed, study, kind, operation, epoch) per call, so "targets", "suppliers" and "band_order" are independent streams rather than continuations of one. Changing how many variates a single operation consumes therefore cannot perturb any other stream's values. This is why a scalar-versus-vector jitter change is a strictly local edit, and it is the general reason this campaign can alter draw counts without side effects on supplier or band-order selection.

5. EPOCH-DEPENDENCE OF A RUN-LEVEL SCALAR IS VERIFIABLE WITHOUT A REPLAY. Drawing u = default_rng(operation_seed(seed, study, kind, "targets", epoch)).random() for epochs 0-9 at a fixed (study, kind) gives ten distinct values, which establishes that a single shared offset still leaves training selection stochastic across epochs and changed_training_studies non-empty. Cheap static check worth running for any mechanism that reduces per-epoch entropy.

6. PROCESS RULE NOW BINDING, STATED AT THE SECOND OCCURRENCE. Two consecutive completions (cross-slot-band-interleaving, and earlier geometry-informed-plane-widths in a weaker form) left their mechanism-level verification condition without a recorded artifact, so their nulls could only be read as nulls of the implemented TARGET-level intervention, not of the realized effect. For this candidate the realized-exposure read-back (min-gap p10/median against the incumbent's ~3.4 / ~8.0 mm, distinct realized middles per pool over ten epochs) is attached as MANDATORY RECORDED REPORTING rather than optional diagnostics. A verification condition attached at approval must produce an artifact; otherwise the completion must explicitly record the mechanism claim as unverified.

7. THE SEALED BASELINE VALIDATION IDENTITY IS AGAIN THE EXPECTED LAUNCH CHECK. 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059. This candidate touches only the `if training` arm and leaves the literal 0.5 branch untouched, so it is the second candidate predicted to reproduce that hash bit-for-bit. Note the standing distinction, which must not be conflated: preprocessing_identity WILL differ (SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit, sampling.py in PREPROCESSING_SOURCES), so the comparison still needs --allowed-input-identity-differences preprocessing_identity, while the validation SELECTION hash must be unchanged.

8. COUNT PRESERVATION REMAINS CONDITIONAL ON THE JOINT LAW. The revision-61 empirical invariance (exactly 93,846 triplets in each of the control's ten epochs) covers the incumbent's own draw distribution only. This candidate changes that distribution, so it does not inherit the invariance and must establish counts in its replay. Supporting expectation, not a substitute: linear_sum_assignment over pools of mean capacity 36.3 against budgets of 5-6 is far from Hall-tight, two of two joint-law-changing candidates so far realized zero shortfall, and a more regular target set is if anything easier to satisfy over discrete geometry groups.

9. Standing items carried forward unchanged: sampler changes must live in modules listed in identity.PREPROCESSING_SOURCES (data/preprocessing/*.py and data/dataset.py in; data/triplet_sampling/ out, and the inert empty scaffold has now been removed from this workspace); realized depth histograms from the CPU replay, never target distributions, are the evidence for shape- or coverage-motivated candidates, because geometry_groups plus linear_sum_assignment snap targets onto existing discrete candidate slices; all cache-derived motivating statistics in this campaign come from the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort and have never been re-measured on the training cohort; the draw-shape family is closed by three nulls and the cross-slot content family by a fourth; both imported ideas are owned by this campaign and docs/backlog/untried-ideas.md entries are read-only provenance; the old 0.003/0.008 single-run heuristics remain superseded by the sealed paired-bootstrap rule; and the comparison tool's limitations (one training seed, one development fold, intervals excluding training-seed and checkpoint-selection variability, allowed config and identity differences moving together) remain the honest frame for every decision here.

2. Diff content, read directly. Two hunks: SAMPLING_IDENTITY["targets"] gains the "_common_bag_offset" suffix, and `rng("targets").random(count)` becomes `rng("targets").random()`. Nothing else. geometry_groups, union_support, study_budgets, largest_remainder, the entitlement/supplier loop and the linear_sum_assignment stage are byte-identical to parent_source.

3. Mechanism verified by execution in the supplied venv, not by inspection. For count in {1,5,6} and u in {0, 1e-9, 0.37, 0.9999999}, every percentile satisfies i/count <= p_i < (i+1)/count, so the incumbent equal-mass band partition is preserved exactly and no band's support changes; adjacent gaps are exactly 1/count (np.allclose), i.e. a rigid lattice, which is the intended mechanism. Per-band marginal: (i+u)/count with u~U(0,1) is exactly Uniform(i/count,(i+1)/count); numerically band 0 over 200,000 draws spans [1.1e-07, 0.1666661] with mean 0.083213 against the exact 1/12 = 0.083333. Incumbent-degeneration idiom (now required, confirmed a fifth time): u = 0.5 reproduces the validation lattice (arange(count)+0.5)/count exactly.

4. Validation independence from the training seed. The non-training branch is the untouched literal 0.5; the whole change is confined to the `if training` arm, and u derives from operation_seed(training_seed,...) which is unreachable when training_seed is None. Deterministic validation selection is therefore bit-identical to the incumbent, and this is the second candidate in the campaign predicted to REPRODUCE the sealed baseline validation selection_sha256 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059. Any deviation is a hard failure of the stated mechanism, not a new recordable identity.

5. Training stochasticity preserved, verified by execution. Because `rng` constructs a fresh default_rng per operation name with the epoch in the payload, u varies per epoch: ten distinct values over epochs 0-9 for a fixed (study, kind). Drawing one scalar instead of `count` cannot perturb any other stream, since "suppliers" and "band_order" are separately keyed generators, not continuations of the same stream. changed_training_studies must therefore stay non-empty and the ten per-epoch training hashes must differ.

6. Count accounting. Structurally, `count = min(budget, len(groups))` is untouched and `percentiles` retains length `count`; targets feed only target_depths. But the candidate deliberately changes the per-epoch JOINT law (bands become perfectly correlated instead of independent within a bag), so the revision-61 empirical invariance is NOT inherited and count preservation must be established by the replay, exactly as the developer states rather than glosses. Mitigating expectation, not a substitute for the check: regularized targets are strictly easier to satisfy over discrete geometry groups, and two of two joint-law-changing candidates so far realized zero shortfall against mean pool capacity 36.3 versus budgets of 5-6.

7. Leakage, coverage, exploitation, cost. No label, fold, cohort, grouping, scoring or checkpoint-selection code touched; no validation-derived feedback; no persistent precomputation or extra decode pass. Coverage is preserved by the exact per-band marginal (measured 23.63 vs 23.73 distinct geometry groups per pool over ten epochs at proposal time); peripheral bands are drawn exactly as often as today. Cost is strictly BELOW the incumbent: one scalar RNG draw replaces `count` draws, and the vectorized truncnorm.ppf is untouched, so the standing per-element-loop budget rule is satisfied with margin against the ~233 s headroom.

NON-BLOCKING OBSERVATIONS (neither justifies the one allowed repair): (a) the per-epoch draw's entropy falls from `count` scalars to one, so the bag centroid wobbles coherently across epochs — this is the idea's own stated counter-hypothesis (within-bag clumping as a regularizer) and makes both outcomes informative; (b) the developer ran no replay, no GPU work and no accounting.calibrate(), and said so rather than claiming trained-model quality, which is the correct scope for this role.

## review-20260919T194446Z-5e413509

Durable findings from this review, for prior-work.md.

1. THE REBASE CHANGES WHAT THE MECHANISM IS, AND THIS IS THE MOST IMPORTANT RECORD FROM THIS EVENT. Against the un-stratified baseline, a shared scalar offset made the bag a rigid Cranley-Patterson rotation of the equal-mass lattice, with adjacent gaps exactly 1/count. Against the accepted run-level-stratified incumbent it cannot be that, because each band keeps its own independently permuted sub-stratum: the shared offset now rotates only WITHIN each band's sub-band. Verified by execution at count=6, E=10 over 20,000 draws: the candidate imposes a hard floor on the minimum adjacent-target gap at exactly 1/(count*E) = 0.01667 in percentile units (measured min 0.01667, p10 0.03333, median 0.08333), while the incumbent's independent per-band jitter has no floor at all. The bulk of the distribution is nearly unchanged; the intervention is now a tail-only removal of residual clumping. Consequence to carry: the motivating +0.00209 frozen-model bag-quality figure was measured against the UN-stratified baseline and very likely overestimates this arm's effect against the current incumbent. General rule: when an accepted mechanism and a queued mechanism act on the same expression, the queued idea's motivating effect size must be re-derived against the new incumbent, not carried over — a rebase can preserve an idea's mechanism while shrinking its headroom.

2. AN ACCEPTED-MECHANISM REBASE IS VERIFIABLE AS A THREE-PART IDENTITY, AND THAT IS THE TEMPLATE. This rebase was checked as: (a) the accepted component reproduces bit-for-bit — the "strata" stream recomputed independently in candidate and incumbent form is np.array_equal on every epoch and count, so the winner's behaviour is preserved rather than silently reverted; (b) the new component is confined — every percentile stays strictly inside its incumbent 1/count band, so supports and marginals are untouched; (c) the incumbent-degeneration identity is demonstrated against the NEW expression — at count=1 the scalar and vector draws agree to np.allclose across all epochs. Part (a) is the new requirement and the one that would have caught the revision-79 invalidation automatically. Any future candidate composing with an accepted mechanism should be held to all three.

3. THE INCUMBENT-DEGENERATION IDIOM IS NOW CONFIRMED SIX TIMES AND, FOR THE FIRST TIME, AGAINST A WINNER RATHER THAN THE BASELINE. Equal side widths (coronal split-normal), band_role None (cross-slot parity), stratification_depth 1 (run-level strata), u = 0.5 (shared rotation vs the deterministic lattice), and now count = 1 against the stratified incumbent expression. Once the incumbent is no longer the sealed baseline, the degeneration check must be run against the incumbent's expression; checking against baseline/source is no longer sufficient evidence.

4. A CANDIDATE CAN BE STRICTLY CHEAPER THAN ITS INCUMBENT AND STILL NEED A COUNT REPLAY. Benchmarked per-call delta is -0.012 us (one scalar RNG draw replacing `count` draws; the vectorized truncnorm.ppf untouched; preparation_seconds 0.11936, i.e. no added preparation cost). Cost and count-risk are orthogonal: the per-epoch JOINT law still changes, so the revision-61 empirical invariance is not inherited and counts must be established in the replay. Supporting expectation only: four of four candidates measured so far realized zero shortfall, and linear_sum_assignment over pools of mean capacity 36.3 against budgets of 5-6 is far from Hall-tight; a more regular target set should if anything reduce shortfall risk.

5. THE SECOND CANDIDATE PREDICTED TO PRESERVE THE VALIDATION IDENTITY, AND THE REASON IS STRUCTURAL. ## review-20260919T195817Z-28945fb5

Durable findings from this review, for prior-work.md.

1. PEAK GPU MEMORY IS NOW A BINDING CAMPAIGN CONSTRAINT, AND THE NUMBERS SHOULD BE CARRIED FORWARD. The incumbent's measured peak CUDA allocation is 20.83 GB (run-level-stratified-jitter training/fold_0/timing.json) on a 24 GB RTX 3090 (docs/architecture/environment.md:48), with model.checkpoint_backbone false so activations dominate and scale near-linearly in token count, and with docs/validation/results/crops-and-views/2026-09-15-two-crops-result.md:25 recording 24.36 GB as "at the RTX 3090's limit, so any larger per-step input needs a smaller physical batch". trainer.batch_size and accumulate_grad_batches are frozen recipe and cannot absorb an increase. Consequence: any mechanism that raises per-epoch triplet counts has roughly +15% of peak (about 3 GB) of usable headroom, i.e. at most about +3 to +4 triplets per study per epoch at the current realized mean of 26.79. This retires the assumption, implicit in earlier rankings, that count-neutral-over-the-run implies resource-neutral: memory and wall clock are per-epoch quantities while the sealed count gate is a run total.

2. THE ACTUAL BUDGET ARITHMETIC, verified from source rather than from docs. base = kind.max_images//3 = (6,6,5,5,5,5), sum 32, and study_triplet_total 26 NEVER rescales it, because study_budgets applies `total` only when counts.sum() < total (sampling.py:427). The realized mean is 938,460/35,030 = 26.79 triplets per visit, the gap to 32 coming from absent or unusable slots. So a +1 offset on every slot is +6 triplets, +18.8% of the nominal 32 and about +22% of the realized mean. Record these three numbers together; the campaign has repeatedly quoted "+19% tokens" without the denominator.

3. ASYMMETRIC PACING SCHEDULES CARRY A ONE-SIDED COUNT RISK THAT SYMMETRIC MECHANISMS DO NOT. A -1 epoch requests strictly less and can never overshoot, so any shortfall in a +1 epoch lowers the run total below the sealed 938,460 with nothing to compensate it, failing the zero-tolerance gate. Every prior candidate could only fail the count gate through a shortfall that the gate would catch symmetrically; a paced schedule converts a single assignment miss into a guaranteed contract failure. Eligibility must therefore guarantee slack in the LARGE epochs (margin = max(schedule)), and the replay is the only place this can be established. Related: `shortfall` is now reported against effective_budget rather than budget, which is the right bookkeeping but means the metadata field no longer compares across epochs.

4. CHANGING BAG SIZE CHANGES BAND WIDTHS, SO IT INTERACTS WITH THE ACCEPTED RUN-LEVEL STRATIFICATION EVEN WHEN THE CODE IS PRESERVED VERBATIM. Verified by execution that np.random.Generator.permuted(axis=1) consumes state row by row, so strata(count=5) and strata(count=7) at the same seed are bit-identical on rows 0-4: the accepted sub-stratum VALUES survive a bag-size change for shared band indices. But bands are 1/count wide, so a paced slot's band i is [i/5,(i+1)/5) in small epochs and [i/7,(i+1)/7) in large ones, and each band's without-replacement sub-stratum sequence no longer refers to a fixed interval over the run. The accepted per-epoch joint law is preserved in form while the run-level coverage property is weakened for paced slots. General rule: preserving an accepted mechanism's code is not the same as preserving its property; when a new mechanism changes a quantity the accepted mechanism is parameterized by (here `count`), the interaction must be stated explicitly and confined as far as the idea allows.

5. AN EPOCH-INVARIANT ELIGIBILITY PREDICATE IS THE RIGHT WAY TO MAKE A PER-EPOCH POLICY EXACTLY RUN-TOTAL-NEUTRAL, AND IT IS CHEAPLY VERIFIABLE. Keying eligibility only on len(groups) (deterministic per pool, computed before any RNG use) and on max(schedule) makes the predicate constant across epochs, so an eligible slot realizes exactly 10*budget and an ineligible slot is bit-identical to the incumbent. Enumerating the real expression over budgets {5,6} and len(groups) in {budget-1, budget, budget+1, 20} confirmed the ten-epoch request total equals 10*min(budget, len(groups)) in every case. Reusable pattern for any future per-epoch allocation policy; verify it by enumeration over the capacity boundary, not by argument, because the interesting cases are exactly at len(groups) == budget and budget+1.

6. A ZERO-SUM VALIDATOR IS NOT A LENGTH VALIDATOR. The implemented model_validator enforces sum(schedule) == 0 but not len(schedule) == max_epochs; with epoch % len(schedule), a schedule whose length does not divide the frozen epoch count would be zero-sum yet NOT count-neutral over the run. Here len 10 == max_epochs 10 so the run total is exact, but future per-epoch schedules must tie their length to the frozen schedule or validate divisibility.

7. DYNAMIC PADDING MEANS COUNTS CANNOT BE SILENTLY CLIPPED, WHICH NARROWS THE CONTRACT RISK TO MEMORY ALONE. dataset.py:315 computes max_slices = max over the batch's entries and dataset.py:323 sizes slice_mask from it, so there is no fixed token capacity that would drop triplets when bags grow; the earlier concern that exceeding a padding capacity would put the counts themselves at risk does not apply to this pipeline. The failure mode of a larger bag is an out-of-memory abort, not a miscount — and an abort mid-run fails the count gate anyway.

8. PROCESS: A PRE-STATED "MEASURE, DO NOT ASSUME" CONDITION IS PART OF THE COMPLETION CONDITION, NOT A DIAGNOSTIC. This candidate satisfied every static check it ran and still could not be approved, because the two quantities the idea itself named as launch preconditions (peak activation memory in the large-bag epochs, total wall clock against the ceiling) were left unverified and the orchestrator's own arithmetic indicates the memory one probably fails. Where a developer role genuinely cannot run the measurement, the substitute is recorded arithmetic against known measured anchors (peak allocation, device capacity, token ratio with its denominator), not deferral to the supervisor. Sibling process item, now at its fourth consecutive occurrence: the realized-exposure read-back attached at approval has still never produced an artifact, and enforcement belongs at the replay step.

9. Standing items carried forward unchanged: sampler changes must live in modules listed in identity.PREPROCESSING_SOURCES (data/preprocessing/*.py and data/dataset.py in; data/triplet_sampling/ out); gates.py allowlists configuration keys under the data.triplet_sampling. prefix and trainer.* remains off limits; any SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit makes preprocessing_identity differ, which is compatible with an UNCHANGED validation selection hash and must not be conflated with it; the sealed validation selection identity is 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059; accounting.py derives selection_sha256 from the sorted (series_type_index, selected paths) rows and never reads metadata, so diagnostic metadata keys are free to add; TripletLedger.observe counts from batch["slice_mask"].sum(dim=1), so the gate measures real selections with padding excluded; realized depth histograms from the CPU replay, never target distributions, are the evidence for coverage- or regularity-motivated candidates; every policy must expose a parameter value at which it provably degenerates to the incumbent, checked by execution against the INCUMBENT's expression (here bag_size_pacing = (), the seventh confirmation); express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call; all cache-derived motivating statistics come from the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort and have never been re-measured on the training cohort; the draw-shape family (three nulls) and the cross-slot content family (one null) are closed; stratification_depth is pinned at 10 and must not be swept; the single acceptance is provisional at a +0.00025 lower bound, and the comparison tool's limitations (one training seed, one development fold, intervals excluding training-seed and checkpoint-selection variability, allowed config and identity differences moving together) remain the honest frame for every decision here.

THE REPAIR, AND WHY IT IS REQUIRED RATHER THAN A NOTE.

The idea's own launch precondition, restated verbatim at every ranking since revision 63, is that peak activation memory in the large-bag epochs and total wall clock against the 1,785 s ceiling must be MEASURED, not assumed, before any launch request. The developer's constraint_evidence explicitly lists both as not verified and out of scope. That alone leaves the completion condition unmet, and my own arithmetic indicates the memory condition does not merely lack evidence but probably FAILS:
- Base budgets are max_images//3 = (6,6,5,5,5,5), sum 32, and study_triplet_total 26 never rescales them (study_budgets only applies `total` when counts.sum() < total). Realized mean is 938,460/35,030 = 26.79 triplets per visit.
- Mean pool capacity is 36.3 against budgets of 5-6, so nearly every slot is eligible and epochs 5-9 carry +6 triplets per fully-present study: +18.8% on the base 32, ~+22% on the realized mean.
- The incumbent's measured peak is 20.83 GB (run-level-stratified-jitter training/fold_0/timing.json) on a 24 GB RTX 3090 (docs/architecture/environment.md:48). model.checkpoint_backbone is false, so activations dominate and scale near-linearly in token count. docs/validation/results/crops-and-views/2026-09-15-two-crops-result.md:25 records 24.36 GB as "at the RTX 3090's limit, so any larger per-step input needs a smaller physical batch".
- Projected peak in the +1 epochs is therefore ~23.7-24.7 GB depending on the activation share (70-95%), i.e. at or above the limit. batch_size and accumulate_grad_batches are frozen recipe and cannot absorb it. Worse, the +1 block is the LATE block, so an OOM would abort at epoch 5 after most of the ceiling is spent, and an aborted run fails the zero-tolerance count gate outright.

CONCRETE REPAIR (bounded, inside the idea's stated design space — its budget section already says "a smaller offset or a subset of slots may be needed" and open_choices names "which slots carry it (memory headroom decides)"). Confine the offsets to a fixed subset of slots such that the worst-case per-study token increase in any epoch is at most +2 triplets (≈ +6-7% of the realized 26.79, projecting ≈ +1.3-1.5 GB to ≈ 22.2-22.4 GB, comfortably under both 20.83 GB + headroom and the documented 24.36 GB regime).
