## review-20260919T200853Z-10646b9e

Durable findings from this review, for prior-work.md.

1. THE MEMORY CEILING HAS NOW ACTUALLY SHAPED A MECHANISM, AND THE CONVERSION ARITHMETIC IS WORTH KEEPING. Confining epoch pacing to a fixed two-slot subset caps the worst-case per-study token increase at exactly +2 triplets (verified by summing effective_budget - budget over the paced slots at the high epoch), i.e. 2/32 = 6.25% of the nominal per-study total and 2/26.79 = 7.46% of the realized run mean 938,460/35,030. Against the incumbent's measured peak 20.83 GB with checkpoint_backbone false (activations dominate, near-linear in token count) and an activation share of 0.70-0.95, that projects to ~21.7-22.3 GB on the 24 GB device, against the documented 24.36 GB at-the-limit regime. The general recipe: per-study token ratio x measured peak x activation share, with the realized mean as the denominator rather than the nominal budget sum, and with trainer.batch_size / accumulate_grad_batches unavailable as absorbers because they are frozen recipe.

2. A MEMORY-DRIVEN CONFINEMENT BUYS FEASIBILITY BY SPENDING REACH, AND THE TRADE MUST BE PRICED BEFORE THE RESULT IS READ. The paced subset here is config-order slots 0 and 1, the two Axial contrasts. The campaign's own measurement (both-slots-present shares ~20% axial / 75% coronal / 89% sagittal) implies most studies carry only ONE of the two paced slots, so the realized per-study swing is nearer +/-1 than the +/-2 worst case and the cohort-average swing is smaller still. Consequence for interpretation, recorded in advance rather than after a null: this arm bounds the value of bag-size pacing at roughly half its nominal amplitude on one plane, not per-epoch pacing in general. Any future candidate confined for feasibility reasons must state its realized reach, not only its worst-case bound, and the replay's per-epoch triplet row vector divided by the visit count is the measurement that settles it.

3. COUNT NEUTRALITY UNDER A PER-EPOCH POLICY IS VERIFIABLE BY ENUMERATION OVER THE CAPACITY BOUNDARY, AND THAT IS THE STANDARD. Enumerating the real expression over budgets {5,6} and len(groups) in {budget-1, budget, budget+1, budget+2, 20} across all ten epochs showed the ten-epoch request total equals 10*min(budget, len(groups)) in every case, with unpaced slots bit-identical and the empty schedule degenerating exactly. The interesting cases are precisely at len(groups) == budget (margin unmet, slot ineligible in all epochs, degenerates trivially) and len(groups) == budget + max(schedule) (first eligible capacity), so an enumeration that omits the boundary proves nothing. An epoch-invariant eligibility predicate keyed only on deterministic pool geometry is what makes the run total exact by construction rather than by tuning.

4. A ZERO-SUM SCHEDULE IS ONLY RUN-TOTAL-NEUTRAL IF ITS LENGTH DIVIDES THE FROZEN EPOCH COUNT, AND THE IMPLEMENTATION SHOULD BE CHECKED FOR THAT SEPARATELY FROM THE VALIDATOR. The model_validator enforces sum == 0 but not len == max_epochs; with `epoch % len(schedule)` a zero-sum schedule of non-dividing length would be accepted and would NOT be count-neutral. Here len 10 == max_epochs 10, confirmed by resolving the full config, so the run total is exact. Future per-epoch schedules must either tie their length to the frozen schedule or validate divisibility explicitly.

5. SHORTFALL ASYMMETRY, STATED CORRECTLY. Because count = min(effective_budget, len(groups)) and shortfall = effective_budget - len(selected) is non-negative, overshoot is impossible in any epoch; therefore any shortfall in a +1 epoch lowers the run total below the sealed 938,460 with nothing to compensate it, hard-failing the zero-tolerance gate. The correct supporting argument is that geometry_groups and linear_sum_assignment are pure functions of (pool, count, targets) and never observe how effective_budget was produced, so a paced slot's shortfall risk at a given effective_budget is identical to that of running the same value as a static budget. Correction to a tempting but loose claim: low (-1) epochs are NOT literally a subset of the incumbent's zero-shortfall regime, because a smaller count changes the band widths and hence the target positions; they are a strictly easier instance (fewer targets over the same geometry groups), so the risk is bounded by, not contained in, the incumbent's. Only the replay settles it.

6. CHANGING BAG SIZE CHANGES BAND WIDTHS, CONFIRMED AGAIN AGAINST THE WINNER'S CODE. np.random.Generator.permuted(axis=1) consumes state row by row, so strata(count=5) and strata(count=7) at the same seed are array-equal on rows 0-4 and the accepted sub-stratum VALUES survive for shared band indices; but bands are 1/count wide (0.200 in low epochs, 0.143 in high), so a paced slot's band index no longer denotes a fixed physical interval across the run. Preserving an accepted mechanism's code is not the same as preserving its property whenever the new mechanism changes a quantity the accepted mechanism is parameterized by. Here the interaction is confined to 2 of 6 slots rather than all 6, which is a second independent benefit of the confinement in finding 1.

7. METADATA FIELDS REBASED ON A NEW DENOMINATOR ARE SAFE ONLY IF NOTHING CONSUMES THEM — CHECK, DO NOT ASSUME. `shortfall` was re-expressed against effective_budget rather than budget, which is correct bookkeeping but silently breaks cross-epoch comparability of the field. Verified by grep that no module outside the sampler reads it, and that accounting.py:78 derives selection_sha256 from the sorted (series_type_index, selected paths) rows without touching selection.metadata, so diagnostic keys remain free to add. The same check should be run for any field whose meaning a candidate redefines.

8. A PERMISSIVE CONFIG FIELD CAN BE INERT RATHER THAN WRONG. bag_size_pacing_slots accepts indices beyond len(series_types) (verified: value (99,) is accepted); such an index simply never matches the runtime loop index, so it is a no-op rather than a fault, and the resolved configuration uses in-range values. Recorded so the absence of a range validator is not later mistaken for a gap in count accounting.

9. PROCESS, CONFIRMED BY THIS REPAIR. The previous candidate failed review solely because two quantities the idea itself named as launch preconditions were deferred to the supervisor; the repair passed by substituting RECORDED ARITHMETIC against known measured anchors (peak allocation, device capacity, token ratio with its denominator) plus enumeration of the real expression. That substitution is the right standard for a role that cannot run GPUs: measurement where possible, arithmetic against measured anchors where not, never assertion. The sibling item is now at its fifth consecutive occurrence: the realized-exposure read-back attached at approval has still never produced an artifact, and enforcement belongs at the supervisor's replay step.

10. Standing items carried forward unchanged: sampler changes must live in modules listed in identity.PREPROCESSING_SOURCES (data/preprocessing/*.py and data/dataset.py in; data/triplet_sampling/ out); gates.py allowlists configuration keys under the data.triplet_sampling. prefix and trainer.* remains off limits; any SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit makes preprocessing_identity differ, which is compatible with an UNCHANGED validation selection hash and must not be conflated with it; the sealed validation selection identity is 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059; TripletLedger.observe counts from batch["slice_mask"].sum(dim=1) and padding is dynamic (dataset.py:315), so the gate measures real selections and no fixed capacity can silently clip a larger bag — the failure mode of a larger bag is an OOM abort, not a miscount; realized depth histograms from the CPU replay, never target distributions, are the evidence for coverage- or regularity-motivated candidates; every policy must expose a parameter value at which it provably degenerates to the INCUMBENT's expression, checked by execution (now eight times, three of them against a winner); express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call; all cache-derived motivating statistics come from the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort and have never been re-measured on the training cohort; the draw-shape family (three nulls) and the cross-slot content family (one null) are closed; stratification_depth is pinned at 10 and must not be swept; the single acceptance is provisional at a +0.00025 lower bound, and the comparison tool's limitations (one training seed, one development fold, intervals excluding training-seed and checkpoint-selection variability, allowed config and identity differences moving together) remain the honest frame for every decision here.

2. WITHIN-BAG COUPLING ADDS NOTHING ONCE RUN-LEVEL STRATIFICATION IS IN PLACE, AND THE TWO MECHANISMS ARE NOT ADDITIVE. The campaign's one acceptance came from coupling ACROSS epochs at a fixed per-epoch joint law (+0.0035). Coupling WITHIN the bag, applied on top of it, returned -0.0031. Both act on the same expression and both regularize the realized depth set, so the natural prior that "more regularity is better" is now falsified as a composition rule: the accepted run-level stratification had already removed most of the clumping, and the residual tail-only gap floor (1/(count*E) = 0.01667 in percentile units, verified at review) bought nothing and plausibly cost something by reducing per-epoch within-band entropy from `count` scalars to one. Working conclusion: regularity is valuable at the level of which sub-intervals are visited over the run, not at the level of rigidly locking a single bag's targets together. The idea's own counter-hypothesis — that independent within-bag jitter acts as a useful regularizer — is now the better-supported reading, though not established by an interval strictly below zero.

3. THE PRE-REGISTERED DOWNWARD RE-DERIVATION WAS CORRECT, AND IT IS THE PROCESS RESULT WORTH KEEPING. At review (revision 89, finding 1) the rebase was shown to convert this arm from a rigid Cranley-Patterson rotation of the count-band lattice into a shared rotation within each already-stratified sub-band, with the bulk of the gap distribution nearly unchanged and only the extreme tail affected; the motivating +0.00209 frozen-model bag-quality figure was explicitly flagged as an overestimate because it had been measured against the UN-stratified baseline. RULE, now evidence-backed: when an accepted mechanism and a queued mechanism act on the same expression, the queued idea's motivating effect size must be re-derived against the new incumbent before launch, and a rebase can preserve an idea's mechanism while destroying its headroom. ## review-20260919T201910Z-a55b780a

Durable findings from this review, for prior-work.md.

1. THE STRONGEST COUNT-PRESERVATION ARGUMENT THE CAMPAIGN HAS FOUND, AND IT IS STRUCTURAL RATHER THAN EMPIRICAL. Supplier preference cannot change realized triplet counts at all. `preferred` enters the cost matrix only additively, as `(value.uid != preferred[row]) * change_cost`, while the existence of an entry in `alternatives` depends solely on `low <= target <= high` over each geometry group's values — a function of targets and groups alone. Since `missing_cost = count*(change_cost+1)+1` strictly exceeds the maximum achievable full-cardinality cost (each real cell costs at most change_cost + 1 after the error/scale normalization), linear_sum_assignment always maximizes cardinality, and that cardinality is determined by the sparsity pattern, not the costs. Therefore any candidate that changes ONLY supplier preference, leaving targets untouched, realizes exactly the incumbent's per-epoch counts. Generalization worth carrying: the campaign's count risk lives entirely in the target-placement stage; interventions downstream of `targets` are count-safe by construction. `scale = max(errors)` does shift when preference changes (because the per-group `best` depends on `wanted`), but a global rescaling of finite costs cannot alter cardinality.

2. A THIRD DESIGN PATTERN FOR MACHINE-CHECKED VALIDATION INDEPENDENCE: COLLAPSING A TERNARY ONTO ITS VALIDATION ARM. Prior candidates achieved validation-identity invariance by confining changes to an `if training` block. Here the change is the opposite move — the training arm of a ternary is deleted and the VALIDATION arm becomes unconditional — which is an even stronger guarantee, since the deterministic branch is not merely untouched but is now the only code path. Any change of this shape should predict the sealed 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 bit-for-bit, and that prediction is falsifiable at the replay step. As always, preprocessing_identity still differs from the SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit; the two must never be conflated.

3. GREEDY LARGEST-DEFICIT ASSIGNMENT UNDER A RANDOMIZED VISITATION ORDER: WHAT IS GUARANTEED AND WHAT IS ONLY OBSERVED. Guaranteed: accumulated entitlement is a per-row sum of Fraction(1, len(choices)) and is therefore exactly order-invariant, so the run's implied proportional supplier shares match the deterministic path exactly. Observed but not proven: the realized integer counts were also order-invariant in every enumerated case — 3/3 over six bands with two equally eligible sources across five random orders, and (4,2) against entitlements (7/2, 5/2) under asymmetric eligibility across eight orders — while the band-to-supplier MAPPING differed every time. That combination (fixed shares, stochastic mapping) is exactly what a randomized balanced allocation should deliver, and it is the reusable verification pattern: check the invariant quantity analytically, check the realized integers by enumeration, and record the mapping's variability as the evidence of preserved stochasticity.

4. TIE-BREAK SEMANTICS OF `max` ARE A SILENT QUALITY POLICY, AND HERE THEY ARE THE RIGHT ONE. Python's `max` returns the first maximal element, and `choices` is built by filtering `sources = pool.ranking`, so deficit ties resolve toward originals and newer same-signature retakes. That makes the training path inherit validation's existing ranked tie-break for free, without adding any explicit quality weighting — which is what let this arm isolate balance alone as directed. Worth recording because the same idiom could silently introduce a preference in a future candidate where it is not wanted; the ordering of the iterable passed to `max`/`min` is part of the policy.

5. THE FAILURE SURFACE OF A REMOVED RNG BRANCH SHOULD BE CHECKED, NOT ASSUMED, AND HERE IT IS UNCHANGED. An empty `choices` would raise under the new `max([])` — but it would equally have raised under the removed `integers(0)`, and the deterministic path has always used `max`, so the pipeline's own completed validation passes are evidence the case does not arise. No new crash mode. General rule: when collapsing a branch, enumerate the degenerate inputs of BOTH arms rather than only the surviving one.

6. THE READ-BACK ENFORCEMENT PROBLEM REACHES ITS SIXTH OCCURRENCE, BUT THIS ARM'S VERSION IS THE CHEAPEST YET. Because targets are unchanged by construction, this candidate needs no realized-middle-depth histogram; the required artifact is only the realized per-source selected counts against the exact fractional entitlement on the 660 multi-source pools, plus the deviation against the incumbent's uniform draw. A mechanism whose verification artifact is cheap and whose invariants are held fixed by construction is worth preferring on those grounds alone, independent of its expected effect size — the campaign has now spent five full budgets on arms whose mechanism claims could not be attributed afterwards.

7. REACH SIZING FOR SOURCE-SELECTION MECHANISMS, CARRIED FORWARD. 660 of 4,368 fold-0 pools (15.1%) hold more than one source, covering 45,523 of 161,466 candidates (28.2%); within those pools, source series explain a median 0.051 of per-triplet contribution variance. Single-source pools (84.9%) are bit-identical under any supplier policy. Any future source-selection idea must be sized against these numbers, and the older prototype figure of 4.53 vs 3.30 percentage points of supplier-share deviation must not be reused as an effect-size estimate: it was measured under a different sampler and predates both the accepted run-level stratification and the current incumbent.

8. Standing items carried forward unchanged: sampler changes must live in modules listed in identity.PREPROCESSING_SOURCES (data/preprocessing/*.py and data/dataset.py in; data/triplet_sampling/ out); gates.py allowlists configuration keys under the data.triplet_sampling. prefix and trainer.* remains off limits; the sealed validation selection identity is 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059; accounting.py:78 derives selection_sha256 from the sorted (series_type_index, selected paths) rows and never reads metadata; TripletLedger.observe counts from batch["slice_mask"].sum(dim=1) with dynamic padding (dataset.py:315), so the gate measures real selections and no fixed capacity can silently clip a bag; realized statistics from the CPU replay, never requested/target quantities, are the evidence for any mechanism-level claim; every policy must expose a parameter value at which it provably degenerates to the INCUMBENT's expression, checked by execution (now nine confirmations, four against a winner — here the single-eligible-source pool); express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call, a per-element Python ppf loop having measured +24.6 s of wall clock nearly 1:1 with its CPU cost; peak GPU memory is a first-class feasibility constraint (incumbent 20.83 GB against a 24 GB device, with ~0.5 GB of run-to-run noise, and the documented 24.36 GB at-the-limit regime), though this candidate leaves bag sizes untouched; all cache-derived motivating statistics come from the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort and have never been re-measured on the training cohort; the draw-shape family (three nulls), the cross-slot content family (one null) and the within-bag coupling family (one null) are closed; stratification_depth is pinned at 10 to the frozen ten-epoch schedule and must not be swept; the sole acceptance remains provisional at a +0.00025 lower bound, with a fresh-seed confirmation reserved for a second acceptance stacking on it; and the comparison tool's limitations (one training seed, one development fold, intervals excluding training-seed and checkpoint-selection variability, allowed config and identity differences moving together) remain the honest frame for every decision here.

Reimplementing the exact loop: (a) with two equally eligible sources over 6 bands, five random band orders all produced the balanced 3/3 split matching the deterministic-order result, while the band-to-supplier mapping differed each time — stochastic assignment at fixed shares, exactly the idea's mechanism; (b) with asymmetric eligibility (band 0 only source A, entitlements 7/2 and 5/2), eight random orders all produced (4,2), i.e. realized shares were order-invariant there too; (c) the degenerate single-eligible-source case yields all bands to that source, reproducing the incumbent — the ninth confirmation of the campaign's degeneration idiom and the fourth against a winner's expression. 5. COUNT ACCOUNTING — STRONGER THAN THE DEVELOPER CLAIMED, AND THIS IS THE MAIN CORRECTION FROM THIS REVIEW. The report states this candidate changes the per-epoch joint law and so cannot inherit the revision-61 empirical count invariance. It is right not to assume, but the invariance here is structural and exact, not empirical. `preferred` enters only additively as `(value.uid != preferred[row]) * change_cost` in costs[row,col]; the existence of an entry in `alternatives` depends solely on `low <= target <= high` over the group's values, which is a function of targets and groups alone. Targets are UNCHANGED by this candidate (same strata and targets streams, same epoch keying). So the sparsity pattern of finite real-column costs is bit-identical to the incumbent's, and since missing_cost = count*(change_cost+1)+1 strictly exceeds the maximum achievable full-cardinality cost, the assignment's cardinality is maximal and identical regardless of supplier preference. Realized triplets per (study, kind, epoch) must therefore equal the incumbent's exactly. What changes is WHICH value is picked within each group (the `uid != wanted` term in the per-group best key) and hence which source supplies each band. Note `scale = max(errors)` does shift because `best` depends on `wanted`, but that only rescales costs and cannot change cardinality. The replay remains the confirmation, and it is now expected to be a formality rather than a risk.

6. LEAKAGE, COVERAGE, EXPLOITATION, PREPROCESSING, COST. No label, fold, cohort, grouping, scoring or checkpoint-selection code touched; no validation-derived feedback; no persistent precomputation; no extra decode pass. ## proposals-20260919T202240Z-8049f56b

Durable findings from this proposals event, for prior-work.md.

1. A MECHANISM-FAMILY CLOSURE IS ONLY AS WIDE AS THE LEVER THAT WAS ACTUALLY PULLED, AND THIS CAMPAIGN HAS NOW DEMONSTRATED THE FAILURE MODE. The within-bag coupling family was closed on common-offset-lattice-jitter's -0.0031. Measured this session on the incumbent source and the fold-0 token cache (/tmp/ar-research/, summary.json), that arm changed realized adjacent min-gap from p10 3.345 / median 7.203 mm to 3.348 / 7.210 mm and frozen-model bag quality by +0.00009 (se 0.00008, 24 paired draws) - it was INERT at the realized level. Its null therefore bounds the fine 1/(count*E) within-sub-band offset and nothing else; it never tested within-bag regularity. The decomposition into a coarse stratum index (spans a whole band) and a fine sub-band offset (spans 1.7% of the unit interval) is the right vocabulary, and the two differ in measured effect by 25x (+0.00227 against +0.00009). RULE: before closing a family, record which component of the expression the arm actually moved AT THE REALIZED LEVEL; a null on an inert lever closes nothing. This is the first concrete cost of the five-consecutive-completions failure to produce the realized-exposure read-back - the artifact that would have shown the arm was inert existed only after the fact.

2. SHARING THE COARSE STRATUM INDEX ACROSS A SLOT'S BANDS IS THE MEASURED HIGH-VALUE FORM, AND IT IS SEPARABLE FROM THE CLOSED FINE-COMPONENT SHARING. Realized min-gap rises to p10 8.863 / median 13.395 mm, essentially the deterministic scored bag's 9.007 / 13.376; frozen-model delta +0.00211 (full rigid) or +0.00227 (shared-stratum-only, per-band jitter retained), against a total incumbent-training-to-scored-bag gap of 0.88524 -> 0.88814. So 73% of that gap is within-bag regularity and it is also a train/scored condition mismatch: the model trains on clumped bags and is scored on a perfect lattice. Verified independently by execution on the incumbent expression (sampling.py:480-490): with a scalar `permuted(arange(E))[phase]` and per-band jitter retained, the band partition is exactly preserved for count in {2,5,6} over all ten epochs, adjacent gaps lie within 1/count +/- 1/(count*E), and at count == 1 the scalar permutation is BIT-IDENTICAL to the incumbent's (count,E) permuted row on all ten epochs - the degeneration idiom holds against the winner's expression.

3. WHEN AN IN-FLIGHT ARM COMPLETES, THE "ONE FACTOR FROM THE HEAD" ARGUMENT INVERTS. The researcher recommended the full rigid form (share stratum AND jitter) because it was one factor from the then-in-flight arm. Once that arm returned a negative-leaning null, the full rigid form became the variant that BUNDLES a factor already measured at -0.0031 with the untested factor, while shared-stratum-only became the one orthogonal to the null. Composition arguments must be re-evaluated against completed results, not against the pipeline state at proposal time.

4. RUN-LEVEL COVERAGE IS ESSENTIALLY EXHAUSTED ON THIS FOLD. Distinct realized geometry groups per slot over ten epochs is 27.69 against a hard ceiling of min(10*count, capacity) = 29.70, i.e. 94.2% of achievable, with mean capacity 34.19, mean count 5.54, 55.4 selections per slot per run and 5.38 of 6.17 available groups visited per band. The binding constraint is pool capacity, not the sampler; headroom is +2.0 groups per slot. Do not queue "visit more distinct candidates over the run" mechanisms. This also verifies on realized exposure the accepted run-level-stratified-jitter coverage claim that three completions left unrecorded.

5. CANDIDATE AND SOURCE ADMISSION IS ALREADY MAXIMAL. Across 904 studies and 4,368 present slots, 5,060 of 5,060 source series are admitted; zero dropped. Diagnostics 4,864 physical / 196 excessive_rms (a pool-path label, not a drop), 4,218 physical pools against 150 local, zero plane disagreements, zero no-valid-run sources, zero split sources, zero singleton runs, one series excluded by excluded_series cohort-wide. Any "relax valid_runs / recover discarded sources" idea is empty on this fold.

6. THE TRAINING BAG IS NOT PAIRED ACROSS CONTRASTS AND THE SCORED BAG IS - AN UNNAMED TRAIN/SCORED MISMATCH. Share of one slot's realized middles within 5 mm of a partner-slot middle: 0.926/0.952/0.943 (axial/coronal/sagittal) deterministically, median nearest partner distance 1.04/0.62/0.76 mm; 0.602/0.566/0.576 in training, median 4.55/4.73/4.76 mm. The training figure is an accident of two independent RNG streams keyed on the full (plane, fluid_sensitive, fat_suppression) tuple, not a design choice. Keying the depth streams on the plane alone raises training co-location to 0.913/0.957/0.950 at unchanged per-slot marginals, coverage (28.765 vs 28.764 distinct groups) and counts. Reach: 903 of 904 studies have a paired plane; 72.1% of per-epoch triplets sit in a slot whose partner is present.

7. MEASURED PRIOR FOR THE RESERVED HEAD. Adding the validation-style entitlement-balanced supplier choice and sorted band order on top of a rigid lattice moves frozen-model bag quality +0.00049 (se 0.00007) to 0.88783 against the deterministic scored bag's 0.88814, i.e. supplier policy explains about a sixth of the remaining train/scored gap, and it costs run coverage (distinct groups per slot 26.29 -> 25.42). Consistent with its 15.1% reach: real but small, with a coverage price nobody had priced.

8. DIRECTIONS FALSIFIED OR JUDGED EMPTY THIS SESSION, recorded so they are not re-derived: per-epoch sigma alternation with the run mean preserved (a shape schedule; family bounded at ~0.002 and the run-level marginal cannot be held exactly); equal-candidate-mass bands (same bound, largely subsumed by finding 4); maximin / regularity-aware assignment cost instead of squared target error (placement error is 1.14-1.26 mm median against 15-25 mm band widths, so realized regularity already tracks target regularity); fixed-anchor plus exploratory remainder (uncertain sign, reduces coverage); label-conditioned depth emphasis (permitted by contract as training-only feedback, but writes the label into the input distribution and guarantees a train/scored mismatch - judged harmful, not merely unpromising); globally shared per-epoch rotation across studies (no independent motivation).

9. THE EVIDENCE CLASS'S TRACK RECORD, STATED PRECISELY. Frozen-model bag quality on the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort under three retired sigma-40 checkpoints trained with the un-stratified baseline sampler has now predicted the sign of 1 of 5 campaign arms, and the one success (run-level stratification) was in this same regularity family. It is directional hypothesis generation, never an effect-size estimate, and effects below the ~0.0037 bootstrap interval half-width can be real and still return inconclusive. A training-cohort realized-exposure replay, and any bag-structure measurement against models trained under the CURRENT incumbent, would materially sharpen every ranking in this campaign; neither exists.

10. Standing items carried forward unchanged: sampler changes must live in modules listed in identity.PREPROCESSING_SOURCES; gates.py allowlists keys under the data.triplet_sampling. prefix and trainer.* is off limits; any SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit makes preprocessing_identity differ, which is compatible with an UNCHANGED validation selection hash (92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059) and must not be conflated with it; accounting.py:78 derives selection_sha256 from the sorted (series_type_index, selected paths) rows and never reads metadata; TripletLedger.observe counts from batch["slice_mask"].sum(dim=1) with dynamic padding, so the gate measures real selections; realized statistics from the CPU replay, never requested/target quantities, are the evidence for any mechanism-level claim, and enforcement belongs at the replay step (six consecutive occurrences, one of which has now cost an attribution); every policy must expose a parameter value at which it provably degenerates to the INCUMBENT's expression, checked by execution (ten confirmations, five against a winner); express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call; peak GPU memory is a first-class constraint (20.83 GB against 24 GB, +/-0.5 GB run-to-run); the draw-shape family (three nulls), the cross-slot DE-correlation family (one null) and the fine-component within-bag coupling factor (one null) are closed; stratification_depth is pinned at 10; the sole acceptance remains provisional at a +0.00025 lower bound with a fresh-seed confirmation reserved for a second acceptance stacking on it; and the comparison tool's limitations (one training seed, one development fold, intervals excluding training-seed and checkpoint-selection variability) remain the honest frame for every decision here.

PROPOSALS EVENT. Both proposals accepted. Live queue 2 -> 4, at the refill threshold and not below it, so NO researcher refill is requested; the revision-105 refill has now landed with this event, so none is in flight. Nothing parked. Reserved head unchanged: new evidence reranks only the unclaimed tail.

RULING ON THE OVERLAP, WHICH IS THE ONLY CONSEQUENTIAL DECISION HERE. run-level-rotated-lattice is adjacent to a closed family (within-bag jitter coupling, closed by common-offset-lattice-jitter at -0.0031, interval [-0.00677, +0.00054]) and to an explicit revision-94 prohibition on "a residual-per-band-jitter variant of the shared offset". I accept it anyway, on evidence that did not exist when either was written, and I accept it in the variant that makes the distinction airtight.

The closed arm was measured this session to be INERT at the realized level: realized adjacent min-gap p10 3.345 / median 7.203 mm under the incumbent against 3.348 / 7.210 under the rebased shared-offset expression, frozen-model delta +0.00009 (se 0.00008). This is the realized-exposure artifact five consecutive completions failed to produce, and it is exactly what the revision-99 completion said was missing when it recorded its own null as unattributable. A closure is only as wide as the lever that was actually pulled: the closed arm shared the FINE 1/(count*E) sub-band offset, which spans 1.7% of the unit interval and cannot separate targets. It therefore bounds the value of the fine component and nothing else. It does not bound the coarse stratum index, which spans a whole band and is the component that actually sets realized regularity (+0.00211/+0.00227 frozen-model, min-gap p10 3.35 -> 8.86 mm, essentially the deterministic scored bag's 9.007). Retaining the closure over the coarse component would preserve a conclusion the measurement has retired.

FIXED CONSTRAINT FOR ITS DEVELOPMENT, AND I AM OVERRIDING THE RESEARCHER'S RECOMMENDATION HERE. Implement SHARED-STRATUM-ONLY: share the sub-stratum index s across a slot's bands, and KEEP the incumbent's per-band jitter `rng("targets").random(count)` exactly as it is. Do NOT implement the full rigid form. The researcher preferred full rigid because it is one factor from the in-flight arm, but that arm has since completed, so the relevant relationship is now to its NULL, not to its expression. Full rigid bundles the coarse change with the same fine-component sharing that has already been measured at -0.0031 in training; shared-stratum-only is orthogonal to that null and isolates the one factor whose value is unmeasured. It is also the better-measured variant on the researcher's own numbers (+0.00227 se 0.00030 against +0.00211 se 0.00031) and it leaves the closed arm's expression untouched, so acceptance here cannot be read as reopening it. No second arm; the full rigid form must not be spawned as arm two.

I verified the construction by execution (venv, incumbent source at sampling.py:480-490): with a scalar `rng("strata", cycle).permuted(np.arange(E))[phase]` and per-band jitter retained, for count in {2,5,6} over all ten epochs every percentile stays strictly inside its incumbent 1/count band (band partition exactly preserved, so supports and per-band marginals are untouched) and every adjacent gap lies within 1/count +/- 1/(count*E) (near-perfect regularity, not the exact lattice of the full rigid form). Degeneration at count == 1 is bit-identical to the incumbent: `permuted(arange(E))[phase]` equals `permuted(tile(arange(E),(1,1)),axis=1)[:,phase]` on all ten epochs at a fixed seed, so the campaign's degeneration idiom holds against the WINNER's expression and must be re-demonstrated by the developer. Further constraints: training arm only, the `else` literal `(np.arange(count)+0.5)/count` untouched so 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 is the hard launch check; stratification_depth stays 10 and is not swept; no cross-slot sharing of the stratum (that is proposal 3's factor and must stay isolable); no new config key expected; count preservation must be established in the replay, since the per-epoch joint law changes (bands become coupled in their stratum index).

HONEST LIMIT CARRIED INTO ITS INTERPRETATION: +0.00227 is frozen-model bag quality on the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort under retired sigma-40 checkpoints. That evidence class has now predicted one sign correctly (run-level stratification) and one incorrectly (the closed arm), on top of four shape-family failures, and +0.00227 sits below the ~0.0037 interval half-width. An inconclusive result is a real possibility even if the mechanism works, and the recorded effect must be read as directional hypothesis generation, not as an effect-size estimate.

PROPOSAL 3, plane-coupled-depth-targets, accepted. The closed cross-slot family is DE-correlation (parity interleaving, half-band offsets, partner-quality conditioning, three-slot generalizations); this is the opposite intervention, and the completion that closed the family recorded complementarity as the reading the data favour, which is the hypothesis this tests directly. Its motivation is also different: a measured train/scored structural mismatch (realized co-location 0.602/0.566/0.576 in training against 0.926/0.952/0.943 deterministically) rather than the redundancy statistic that motivated the closed arm. Constraints: share the "targets" and "strata" streams on `(kind[0],)` while leaving "suppliers" and "band_order" keyed on the full `kind`, so the supplier lottery stays independent and the head remains isolable; each slot keeps its own geometry_groups and linear_sum_assignment; training arm only; degeneration is scope == `kind` and must be demonstrated by execution; the unequal-partner-`count` case must be resolved by execution, not assumption. It ranks third because its predicted effect is small and genuinely two-sided (the researcher's own -0.00065 raw bag-quality strike is confounded with the matched-condition effect under test and cannot be trusted either way), while its invariants are strong: each slot's own per-epoch law, coverage, band masses and counts are unchanged by construction, cost is zero, and the validation identity is preserved.

## review-20260919T204733Z-d04e43af

Durable findings from this review, for prior-work.md.

1. A LOW p-VALUE IN A MARGINAL CHECK MUST BE RESOLVED, NOT WAVED THROUGH, AND THE RESOLUTION PROCEDURE IS CHEAP. The candidate's within-band position at band 2 returned KS p 0.042 at 20,000 draws and 0.048 at 60,000, which is exactly the shape of a real but small marginal distortion. Decomposing it settled the question: the shared stratum's own marginal is uniform (chi-square over 60,000 draws, counts 5848-6099, p 0.28), the unchanged jitter stream is uniform (p 0.54), and a systematic sweep of 108 tests (3 seeds x 2 kinds x 3 epochs x 6 bands, 40,000 draws each) gave 6.5% below 0.05 with the p-value distribution itself uniform (KS p 0.16, median 0.50). REUSABLE RULE: a single suspicious p-value on one band at one epoch is not evidence; test the p-value distribution across seeds, kinds, epochs and bands, and decompose the construction into its independent streams. Repeating the same test at a larger N on an OVERLAPPING study set is not independent evidence and will reproduce the same realization.

2. "COUPLED BUT NOT RIGID" IS A MEASURABLE PROPERTY, AND IT IS THE CHECK THAT ENFORCES A SHARED-COARSE-COMPONENT-ONLY CONSTRAINT. Sharing the coarse stratum while retaining per-band jitter gives a max off-diagonal within-band-position correlation of 0.9903 at count 6 (the forbidden full rigid form would give exactly 1.0), and realized adjacent min-gaps just below the 1/count lattice rather than on it (count 6: 0.1541 vs 0.1667; count 5: 0.1849 vs 0.2000; count 2: 0.4750 vs 0.5000), with the 1/(count*E) floor an order of magnitude lower. When a constraint forbids one factor of a two-factor expression, verify the forbidden factor is absent by measuring the quantity it would saturate, not by reading the diff alone.

3. REMOVING A `count` DEPENDENCE FROM AN ACCEPTED MECHANISM'S RNG SHAPE RETIRES A KNOWN FRAGILITY. Revision 61 flagged that the incumbent's (count, E) permutation would silently degrade to a per-cycle re-draw if a slot's realized `count` ever varied between epochs. The scalar `permuted(arange(E))[phase]` does not depend on count at all, so the run-level stratification schedule becomes invariant to count variation. A candidate can strengthen the accepted mechanism it composes with, and that should be recorded as part of the composition check alongside the usual "is the winner preserved" question.

4. THE THREE-PART ACCEPTED-MECHANISM COMPOSITION CHECK (revision 89) EXTENDS TO A FOURTH PART: WHICH PROPERTY OF THE WINNER SURVIVES. Here the accepted property is that every band visits all E sub-strata over the run. Sharing the stratum across bands preserves it exactly (the ten per-epoch shared values are a permutation of 0..9, and every band uses that value), so run-level coverage is untouched while cross-band coupling changes. Contrast with epoch-paced-bag-size, where the winner's CODE was preserved verbatim but its property was weakened because band widths moved. State explicitly which property, not only which code, survives.

5. FOUR CONSECUTIVE CANDIDATES HAVE NOW PREDICTED THE VALIDATION-IDENTITY INVARIANCE IN ADVANCE, AND THREE HAVE CONFIRMED IT BIT-FOR-BIT (92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059). It converts validation seed-independence from an argued property into a machine-checked one and immunizes the arm against the ~0.0006 train/validation matched-condition effect. preprocessing_identity still differs from any SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit; the two must never be conflated.

6. COUNT-PRESERVATION ENTITLEMENTS ARE NOT TRANSFERABLE BETWEEN CANDIDATES. The supplier arm's structural cardinality argument (revision 121: supplier preference enters costs only additively and the sparsity pattern depends on targets and groups alone) does NOT extend to any candidate that moves targets, and the revision-61 empirical invariance covers only the incumbent's own joint law. This candidate moves targets and changes the joint law, so it has neither entitlement and must establish counts in its own replay. The supporting expectation stands but is not a substitute: six of six measured candidates realized zero shortfall, pools average capacity 36.3 against budgets of 5-6, and a more regular target set is strictly easier to satisfy over discrete geometry groups.

7. THE DEGENERATION IDIOM IS NOW CONFIRMED TWELVE TIMES, SEVEN AGAINST A WINNER. This is the "shared coarse component, retained fine jitter" construction the idea specifies, and it leaves the closed fine-component arm's expression untouched, so the approval cannot be read as reopening that closure.

At the incumbent's own seed 4061686476: (a) BAND PARTITION EXACTLY PRESERVED — for count in {2,5,6} over all ten epochs, zero violations of i/count <= p_i < (i+1)/count, so every band's support is unchanged; (b) RUN-LEVEL COVERAGE PRESERVED — the ten per-epoch shared stratum values are exactly a permutation of 0..9 (sequence [6,4,0,5,9,7,2,1,3,8]), and since every band shares that value, each band still visits all ten sub-strata over the run, which is the accepted incumbent's property held fixed; (c) DEGENERATION AT count == 1 IS BIT-IDENTICAL to the incumbent's own expression on all ten epochs (np.array_equal on the full percentile vector) — the twelfth confirmation of the campaign idiom and the seventh against a winner; (d) TRAINING STOCHASTICITY PRESERVED — ten distinct shared strata across epochs 0-9, so changed_training_studies must stay non-empty and the ten per-epoch training hashes must differ.

A first 20,000-draw test showed band 2 at KS p 0.042 and a 60,000-draw test at 0.048, which looked like a possible marginal distortion. I resolved it rather than waving it through: the shared stratum's own marginal is uniform (chi-square over 60,000 draws, counts 5848-6099, p 0.28), the unchanged jitter stream is uniform (p 0.54), and a systematic sweep of 108 tests (3 seeds x 2 kinds x 3 epochs x 6 bands, 40,000 draws each) gives 6.5% of tests below 0.05 with the p-value distribution itself indistinguishable from uniform (KS p 0.16, median 0.50). The low values were sampling noise. CONCLUSION: the per-epoch per-band MARGINAL law is exactly the incumbent's; only the joint across bands changes, which is the mechanism. This is the three-level support/marginal/joint distinction recorded at revision 73, and this candidate holds the first two fixed by construction.

1. RESULT OF RECORD. entitlement-balanced-supplier-draw (the training supplier draw in select_triplets adopts the deterministic validation path's exact-fractional-entitlement, largest-deficit assignment rule under a randomized band order; the "suppliers" RNG stream and its uniform draw are deleted, and the previously inert "band_order" stream now carries the stochastic processing order) is INCONCLUSIVE; the incumbent is retained. Evidence: experiments/entitlement-balanced-supplier-draw-20260919T201634Z-29f0cf3d/comparison/comparison.json.

2. A STRUCTURAL COUNT-PRESERVATION ARGUMENT HAS NOW BEEN CONFIRMED BY MEASUREMENT, AND THIS IS THE MOST REUSABLE RESULT HERE. Revision 121 argued that supplier preference cannot change realized counts at all, because `preferred` enters the cost matrix only additively while the existence of an entry in `alternatives` depends solely on targets and geometry groups, and missing_cost strictly exceeds any achievable full-cardinality cost, so linear_sum_assignment always maximizes a cardinality fixed by the sparsity pattern. The run realized exactly 93,846 triplets in every one of its ten epochs — bit-equal to the incumbent's per-epoch figure, not merely equal in the run total. The general statement now has evidence: the campaign's count risk lives entirely in the target-placement stage, and an intervention strictly downstream of `targets` is count-safe by construction. Candidates that MOVE targets inherit neither this argument nor the revision-61 empirical invariance and must still establish counts in their own replay.

3. SOURCE SELECTION IS THE LAST UNTESTED MECHANISM FAMILY AND IT DID NOT HELP. Eight full-budget arms now decompose as: three density-shape nulls, one cross-slot content null, one within-bag fine-component coupling null, one bag-size pacing null, one source-selection null, and a single acceptance (run-level-stratified-jitter, +0.0035). Every arm that changed WHERE targets sit within a bag, WHAT a bag contains across slots, HOW MANY triplets an epoch carries, or WHICH series supplies a band has returned an interval straddling zero. The only intervention that moved the metric coupled draws ACROSS epochs at a fixed per-epoch joint law. That is now the campaign's single positive result after eight arms, and it is the direct argument for the reserved head (sharing the coarse sub-stratum index across a slot's bands).

4. ALIGNING TRAINING WITH THE DETERMINISTIC SCORING POLICY IS NOT AUTOMATICALLY GOOD, WHICH BOUNDS THE MATCHED-CONDITION HEURISTIC. This arm was motivated by a source-verified train/validation policy mismatch and made training's supplier rule identical to validation's; the point estimate moved down by 0.0029. Combined with cross-slot-band-interleaving (which moved a different matched-condition axis in the de-correlating direction and also returned negative), the honest statement is that the ~0.0006 matched-condition effect documented for sampler width does not generalize into a reliable prior for arbitrary train/scored alignment. Alignment removes a mismatch but also removes training-time diversity; the net sign is an empirical question per mechanism, and the campaign has no arm in which alignment alone has paid.

5. THE MEASURED PRIOR WAS ROUGHLY THE RIGHT SIZE AND THE WRONG SIGN, AGAIN. The researcher measured this policy at +0.00049 (se 0.00007) of frozen-model bag quality — about a sixth of the remaining train/scored gap — with a recorded coverage COST (distinct geometry groups per slot over the run 26.29 -> 25.42). The training outcome was -0.0029. Frozen-model bag quality on the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort under retired sigma-40 checkpoints has now predicted the sign of 1 of 6 campaign arms. It remains directional hypothesis generation only, and where it reports a coverage cost alongside a quality gain, the coverage cost deserves at least equal weight — this arm's null is consistent with the coverage price dominating a small alignment gain.

6. REACH AND EFFECT, PRICED TOGETHER FOR FUTURE SOURCE-SELECTION SIZING. 660 of 4,368 fold-0 pools (15.1%) hold more than one source, covering 45,523 of 161,466 candidates (28.2%); within those pools, source series explain a median 0.051 of per-triplet contribution variance; single-source pools (84.9%) are bit-identical under any supplier policy. An arm confined to that reach produced a point estimate of -0.0029 and a substantial model change (mean absolute probability difference 0.0642, per-target Spearman 0.898-0.964), i.e. narrow reach did NOT imply a small perturbation of the fitted model — the seventh consecutive arm to move the model materially while leaving fold-0 macro AUC inside ±0.0035.

7. THE READ-BACK FAILURE REACHES EIGHT CONSECUTIVE COMPLETIONS, AND THIS ONE COST THE ARM'S MAGNITUDE. Launch condition 3 required recording realized per-epoch supplier shares on the 660 multi-source pools against the exact fractional entitlement, plus the deviation against the incumbent's uniform draw. No artifact exists. Counts and validation identity prove the change took effect, so unlike common-offset-lattice-jitter the null is attributable in direction; what is unknown is the realized share deviation, i.e. how far the training draw actually moved toward the entitlement target. The design implication recorded at revision 133 is reinforced: prefer mechanisms whose realized effect appears in an artifact the harness already emits (per-epoch triplet counts, selection hashes, accounting rows), and where it cannot, treat producing the artifact as part of the launch rather than as diagnostics.

8. PER-TARGET AND FAMILY DIAGNOSTICS (informative only; none individually resolvable, none may justify a retune). Widest reach in the queue (72.1% of per-epoch triplets, 903 of 904 studies) and the cleanest invariants, but a small and genuinely two-sided expected effect whose own frozen-model strike (-0.00065) is confounded with the matched-condition effect under test.
3. physical-span-standardized-triplets — unchanged at last. Only idea changing what a triplet IS, narrowest reach (~6% of pools below 1 mm median spacing), and the only one needing the preparation.py scope widening (granted, limited to preparation.py:125-130 and its immediate callers, both preconditions unchanged: inspect the p1 0.03 mm pools before assuming they are recoverable, and confirm unique-decode counts in the replay). It changes targets AND deterministic selection, so it inherits neither the cardinality argument nor validation-identity invariance and must record a new validation identity.

SURPLUS RETAINED, NOT QUEUED: bimodal / compartment-weighted sagittal draw. The shape family's ~+0.002 bound keeps it out; I am not queueing it to fill depth.

CLOSED DIRECTIONS, extended by this completion: entitlement-balanced-supplier-draw as an implementation and training-supplier-allocation as a mechanism family — no quality-weighted variant (already closed at revision 121), no partial-balance interpolation between the uniform and entitlement rules, no per-plane or per-slot subset of the entitlement rule, and no supplier-side re-run motivated by the unrecorded share read-back. All prior closures stand unchanged: epoch-paced-bag-size and across-epoch-budget-pacing at the amplitudes the resource ceiling permits; common-offset-lattice-jitter and the sharing of the FINE within-sub-band offset (the coarse component is the reserved head; the full rigid form must not be spawned); cross-slot depth DE-duplication / partner-slot decorrelation (entry 2 is the disclosed opposite-direction exception); geometry-informed-plane-widths including the exact ratio variant; coronal-side-relative-draw; learned per-triplet attention feedback; per-study anatomical anchor; supplier-preference-degrading-placement; new stratification axes and run-splitting on this fold; dropout-aware budget redirection (contract-blocked); non-physical/local pools; jitter-amplitude annealing; between-study budget reallocation; per-type centre offsets; common-factor sigma sweeps; fixed-total reallocations; repeated_series_share; side-conditioned sagittal sampling; intra-pool overlap/repulsion; capacity-recovery/shortfall reallocation; stratification_depth sweeps; run-level coverage mechanisms of the "visit more distinct candidates" form; candidate/source admission relaxation; per-epoch sigma alternation; equal-candidate-mass bands; maximin/regularity-aware assignment cost; fixed-anchor-plus-exploratory-remainder; label-conditioned depth emphasis; globally shared per-epoch rotation across studies.

## proposals-20260919T211650Z-a93f05de

Durable findings from this proposals event, for prior-work.md.

1. A NEW FACTOR EXISTS IN THE CAMPAIGN'S ONLY PRODUCTIVE MECHANISM CLASS: THE TIMING OF EXPOSURE AT FIXED CONTENT. Every arm to date has changed what a bag contains, how big it is, which series supplies it, or which set is covered over the run. prefix-balanced-stratum-order changes none of those; it changes only the ORDER in which each band consumes its ten sub-strata. Verified analytically against the incumbent source (sampling.py:480-488): with stratum_i(e) = (r_i + sigma(e)) mod E, r_i independent uniform per band and sigma a fixed permutation, the per-epoch joint law ACROSS BANDS is exactly the incumbent's (a fixed shift of an independent uniform residue is independent uniform), and because sigma is a permutation every band still visits all E sub-strata exactly once over the run, so the accepted incumbent's coverage property is preserved exactly rather than approximately. The measured motivation is entirely temporal: LR-mass-weighted distinct geometry groups per band 3.0765 against 2.9219 (+5.3%), consecutive-epoch same-group rate 4.75% against 15.66% (3.3x lower), full-run distinct per band and per slot identical (5.332 and ~27.0). The LR mass schedule itself is worth recording: per-epoch share [0.108, 0.194, 0.183, 0.161, 0.132, 0.099, 0.066, 0.037, 0.015, 0.004], so epochs 0-4 carry 77.8% and epochs 7-9 carry 5.6% of the run's total learning-rate mass under warmup 1 + cosine to 0.01 over 4380 steps.

2. FIVE DIRECTIONS FALSIFIED BY DIRECT MEASUREMENT THIS SESSION, NOT BY ARGUMENT, ON THE FOLD-0 TOKEN CACHE WITH THE HARNESS REPRODUCING THE CAMPAIGN'S RECORDED REFERENCE POINTS. (a) LOCALLY CLUSTERED / PAIRED / CONTIGUOUS BAGS ARE STEEPLY DOMINATED: the training bag contains no through-plane continuity anywhere (realized adjacent min-gap median 7.2 mm against a 3.3 mm median candidate spacing), and a bag built from stratified anchors each contributing two adjacent candidates scores 0.87742 against 0.88547 at matched counts, i.e. -0.0081. Regularity is not merely preferred, it is steep, and "give the model local continuity somewhere" is closed. (b) PER-EPOCH REALLOCATION BETWEEN SLOTS at an exactly fixed per-study total scores -0.0060; both the static and the temporal forms of between-type reallocation are now empty, and this one was attractive precisely because it was memory-neutral. (c) A FINER RUN-LEVEL TARGET GRID via a rotating dropped band is neutral (+0.00025, inside per-draw spread) and would buy coverage already at 94.2% of the achievable ceiling. (d) NO SNAPPING BIAS EXISTS between intended and realized depth density: over 241,720 emulated targets the realized/intended mean |depth| ratio is 1.0010, sd 26.167 against 26.131 mm, beyond-1.5-sigma fraction 0.0103 against 0.0101, largest per-band mean signed displacement 0.039 mm. Any "correct the realized marginal" or "quantile-match on the candidate lattice" proposal addresses nothing, and the residual gap between realized distinct groups per slot (~27) and pool capacity (~33) is the intended mass weighting, not grid fineness. (e) NO FREE PER-CANDIDATE QUALITY SIGNAL EXISTS BEYOND DEPTH: within-pool z-scored attention share correlates -0.0003 with local slice spacing and -0.005 with valid_tokens, while |depth| (-0.674) and distance-to-run-edge (+0.673) are the same covariate the truncated Gaussian already encodes. This independently reconfirms the quality-weighted selection closure and is a mild negative datum for any spacing-driven proposal. Pool support asymmetry is also not a pathology (median pool loses 6.6% of its half-extent; only 5.7% lose more than 30%).

3. ONE MECHANISM IS MATHEMATICALLY OBSTRUCTED AND MUST NOT BE RE-DERIVED. Run-level stratification of the CROSS-SLOT phase — making a plane's two slots visit all ten relative registration states exactly once over the run — requires a complete mapping (orthomorphism) of Z_E, which exists only for odd E. E is pinned at 10, so only an unbalanced approximation is available and it would destroy the per-band invariant that would have made it worth running.

4. THE MODEL'S POSITION ENCODING IS NOT THE SAMPLER'S COORDINATE, AND THIS CONDITIONS TWO LIVE ARMS. preparation.py:194 takes relative_position_mm, which series.py sets relative to each SERIES' own slab midpoint, while the sampler places targets relative to the fitted STUDY reference. Measured per series the two frames differ by median |offset| 3.21 mm, p90 11.73 mm, with 35.4% beyond 5 mm and 14.3% beyond 10 mm; within multi-source pools the two suppliers' frames differ by median 0.00 mm but p90 15.77 mm and maximum 79.36 mm; across a plane's two contrasts, median 0.00 mm but p90 5.07 mm. (The sagittal sign flip on 96.6% of series is expected and handled by reflection_positions.) RULING: fixing this is a change to the model's input encoding, not to triplet selection, so it is OUT OF SCOPE for this campaign and must not be implemented here. It is IN SCOPE as an interpretation limit and must be carried with plane-coupled-depth-targets, whose mechanism assumes study-frame co-location reaches the model, and with the completed supplier arm, where an epoch-to-epoch supplier switch at fixed study-frame depth can move the encoded position materially. Record it in the campaign documentation for whatever work owns the encoding.

5. A CLOSURE HELD, WITH ITS BOUNDARY STATED. Revision 127 established that a family closure is only as wide as the lever actually pulled, and that principle reopened the coarse stratum component. It does NOT automatically reopen the supplier family for a cross-epoch variant: the lever differs (within-epoch versus cross-epoch balance), but the REACH that bounded the tested arm is identical (15.1% of slots, 28.2% of candidates, median 0.051 of within-pool contribution variance explained by source), and reach, not lever, is what bounds the achievable magnitude. run-level-balanced-supplier-cycling is therefore retained as surplus rather than queued or parked, requeueable only on new evidence that realized cross-epoch source concentration is materially larger than the 10.9%-at-8:2-or-worse figure implies. General rule: when re-opening a closed family, ask whether the new idea escapes the null's LEVER or its REACH; escaping only the lever is not sufficient.

6. THE CHEAP EVIDENCE CLASS IS STRUCTURALLY BLIND TO THE FAMILY THAT PRODUCED THE ONLY ACCEPTANCE. The frozen-model token cache scores single bags, so it cannot speak to any temporal mechanism at all. Consequence, recorded at acceptance rather than after a result: prefix-balanced-stratum-order is the first accepted arm in this campaign with NO bag-quality prior in either direction, and its effect could be zero. Separately, the per-epoch bag-CONTENT axis is now close to fully accounted for — the incumbent training-bag to scored-bag gap is about 0.0029, the shared-stratum arm currently training is measured to close about 73% of it and the completed supplier arm about a sixth, leaving roughly 0.0003 — so any further bag-content proposal is bidding for that remainder and the remaining leverage is temporal.

7. MISSING EVIDENCE, NOW FLAGGED FOR THE THIRD CONSECUTIVE REFILL AND MORE BINDING EACH TIME. Every motivating statistic in this campaign, without exception, comes from the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort; the training cohort's pool geometry has never been measured. All frozen-model figures come from retired sigma-40 checkpoints trained under the un-stratified baseline sampler, an evidence class that has now predicted the sign of 1 of 6 arms. A training-cohort realized-exposure measurement, or any bag-structure measurement against models trained under the current incumbent, would sharpen every ranking here more than another marginal proposal would.

8. Standing items carried forward unchanged: sampler changes must live in modules listed in identity.PREPROCESSING_SOURCES; gates.py allowlists keys under the data.triplet_sampling. prefix and trainer.* is off limits; any SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit makes preprocessing_identity differ, which is compatible with an UNCHANGED validation selection hash (92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059, confirmed bit-for-bit by four consecutive candidates) and must not be conflated with it; accounting.py:78 derives selection_sha256 from the sorted (series_type_index, selected paths) rows and never reads metadata; TripletLedger.observe counts from batch["slice_mask"].sum(dim=1) with dynamic padding, so the gate measures real selections and a too-large bag fails by OOM abort rather than by miscount; the count risk lives entirely in the target-placement stage, so interventions strictly downstream of `targets` are count-safe by construction (confirmed by measurement on the supplier arm) while any candidate that moves targets must establish counts in its own replay; realized statistics from the replay, never requested/target quantities, are the evidence for any mechanism-level claim, and enforcement belongs at the replay step (eight consecutive unrecorded occurrences, one of which cost an attribution outright); every policy must expose a parameter value at which it provably degenerates to the INCUMBENT's expression, checked by execution (twelve confirmations, seven against a winner); express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call, and note that a strictly cheaper sampler is not a faster run — every arm lands +24 to +30 s over the control regardless; peak GPU memory is a first-class feasibility constraint (20.82-21.30 GB against 24 GB, ~0.5 GB run-to-run, 24.36 GB the documented at-the-limit regime); stratification_depth is pinned at 10 to the frozen ten-epoch schedule; the sole acceptance remains provisional at a +0.00025 lower bound with a fresh-seed confirmation reserved for a second acceptance stacking on it; and the comparison tool's limitations (one training seed, one development fold, intervals excluding training-seed and checkpoint-selection variability, allowed config and identity differences moving together) remain the honest frame for every decision here.

PROPOSALS EVENT (researcher-20260919T204527Z-eeeaf8d6). One of two proposals accepted. Live queue 2 -> 3, still below the refill threshold of 4, and the revision-141 refill has landed with this event, so ONE researcher refill is requested now and may run concurrently with the detached run-level-rotated-lattice training and with the head's development. Nothing parked. Reserved head unchanged: new evidence reranks only the unclaimed tail.

STATE READ FROM THE QUEUE, NOT FROM THE REPORTS. run-level-rotated-lattice has left the queue (launched, detached, shared-stratum-only form approved at revision 141). plane-coupled-depth-targets is status developing with experiment plane-coupled-depth-targets-20260919T211410Z-c36f925a, so it is the reserved head and stays first by the reservation rule; it is also the one permitted candidate in development, so entry 2 does not enter development until that launch removes the head.

ACCEPTED: prefix-balanced-stratum-order (mechanism_id within-run-exposure-ordering). Verified the premise directly against the incumbent source rather than the report: sampling.py:480-488 is exactly `strata = rng("strata", cycle).permuted(np.tile(np.arange(E), (count,1)), axis=1)[:, phase]` feeding `(np.arange(count) + (strata + rng("targets").random(count))/E)/count`, with the `else` literal `(np.arange(count)+0.5)/count` untouched at line 490. The proposal's quoted expression, line references and composition claims are accurate.

Why it is accepted, in the campaign's own terms. After nine full-budget arms the single mechanism class that has moved fold-0 macro AUC is coupling ACROSS epochs at a fixed per-epoch marginal (run-level-stratified-jitter, +0.0035). Every other family is closed by a measured null: three density-shape, cross-slot de-correlation, within-bag fine-component coupling, across-epoch budget pacing, and now source selection. This proposal is the only live idea in the productive class that is not already in the pipeline, and it is a genuinely new factor within it: the accepted mechanism fixed WHICH sub-strata a band visits over the run; nothing has ever changed WHEN. I checked the construction analytically rather than accepting the chi-square evidence offered. With stratum_i(e) = (r_i + sigma(e)) mod E and r_i independent uniform per band, the per-epoch joint law ACROSS BANDS is not merely "distributionally identical" but exactly the incumbent's (independent uniform residues), because a fixed shift applied to an independent uniform variable is independent uniform; and because sigma is a permutation of positions, every band still visits all E sub-strata exactly once over the run, so the accepted incumbent's property is preserved exactly, not approximately. That is the strongest invariant set any candidate has carried: per-epoch joint law exact, full-run realized set identical, band partition/support/marginals untouched, counts unchanged, validation branch untouched, cost neutral, and a bit-exact degeneration at E = 1. Both outcomes are therefore cheaply interpretable, which is the criterion the last three rankings have prioritised.

FIXED CONSTRAINTS FOR ITS DEVELOPMENT (creative latitude over implementation details is preserved; these are the mechanism and its boundaries).
1. SHARED-ORDER FORM ONLY, per the researcher's own recommendation and my ruling on open choice (i): one VISIT_ORDER = (stride * arange(E)) % E with stride 3 at E = 10, shared across bands, and an independent uniform rotation r_i per band. Do NOT draw a per-band direction from {3, 7}: the researcher measured the two identically on every statistic, so the extra bit buys nothing and costs one-factor cleanliness. The order is NOT a sweepable parameter; the greedy circular-dispersion alternative was measured and rejected (16.8% consecutive repeats, worse than the incumbent) and must not be spawned as arm two, and neither may any other stride.
2. stratification_depth stays 10 and must not be swept.
3. Training arm only. The `else` literal at sampling.py:490 stays untouched, so the sealed validation selection_sha256 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 must reproduce bit-for-bit on all ten rows, with the ten per-epoch training hashes distinct. Fifth candidate to predict this invariance in advance.
4. DEGENERATION AND JOINT-LAW IDENTITY MUST BE DEMONSTRATED BY EXECUTION AGAINST THE INCUMBENT'S EXPRESSION, not argued: (a) at stratification_depth = 1 the candidate array is bit-identical to the incumbent's for every study, count and epoch; (b) each band's ten-epoch sequence is a permutation of 0..E-1; (c) the per-epoch across-band joint law matches the incumbent's (independent uniform), and the developer should state whether it verifies this analytically or by sampling.
5. PARENT AND REBASE TRIGGERS, ruled here so the developer does not have to guess. Build on the CURRENT incumbent expression (run-level-stratified-jitter, the (count, E) permuted form). Two live arms touch the same stream and are rebase triggers, exactly as common-offset-lattice-jitter was rebased at revision 79: if run-level-rotated-lattice (training now) is accepted, the shared stratum becomes a scalar and this candidate applies unchanged and simpler to that single sequence; if plane-coupled-depth-targets (the head) is accepted, the "strata" stream is keyed on (kind[0],) and this candidate applies to the shared per-plane stream. In either case the accepted component must be preserved bit-for-bit and the composition check of revision 89/141 applies, including which PROPERTY of the winner survives, not only which code.
6. Count preservation must be established in the candidate's own replay. It moves targets, so it inherits neither the revision-61 empirical invariance nor the revision-121 cardinality argument (which covers only interventions strictly downstream of `targets`, now confirmed by measurement on the supplier arm).
7. MECHANISM VERIFICATION AT THE REPLAY STEP, and this is the reason the idea is worth running rather than merely elegant. Its discriminating statistic is per-band distinct-realized-sub-strata AFTER k EPOCHS (2- and 3-epoch prefixes) plus the consecutive-epoch same-geometry-group rate, against the incumbent's emulated 15.66%; full-run distinct realized middles per pool must be UNCHANGED (26.99 emulated). Unlike the two coupling arms that preceded it, the full-run half of this read-back is a quantity the replay already computes. The read-back has now gone unrecorded eight consecutive completions and cost one attribution outright; enforcement belongs at the replay step.

HONEST LIMITS RECORDED WITH THE ACCEPTANCE, not smoothed over. (a) The LR-mass weighting that motivates the arm assumes gradient contribution scales with learning rate; that is a standard proxy and is untested here. (b) This is the first accepted idea in the campaign with NO bag-quality prior in either direction, because the frozen-model token cache scores single bags and this changes nothing about any single bag; its effect could be zero, and +5.3% LR-weighted exposure diversity has no established conversion to macro AUC. (c) The emulation uses the nearest-depth approximation on the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort; the training cohort's pool geometry has still never been measured, which the researcher flags for the third consecutive refill.

NOT ACCEPTED, RETAINED AS SURPLUS, NOT PARKED: run-level-balanced-supplier-cycling. The proposal is well built and its invariants are real, but the supplier family was closed at revision 149 on a measured null (entitlement-balanced-supplier-draw, -0.0029, interval [-0.0065, +0.0007]), and the reach that bounded that arm bounds this one identically: 15.1% of slots, 28.2% of candidates, source series explaining a median 0.051 of within-pool contribution variance. The researcher states plainly that the predicted effect is well under the ~0.0037 interval half-width and ranks it second below its own first proposal. I am not treating the closure as automatically decisive — revision 127 showed a closure is only as wide as the lever actually pulled, and cross-epoch balance is a different lever from within-epoch balance — which is why this is surplus rather than parked. But three of the campaign's nine arms have now been spent on interventions whose predicted magnitude was below the decision rule's resolution, and queueing a fourth on the family that has just returned a negative-leaning null is not the best use of the next full budget. Condition for requeueing: new evidence that the realized cross-epoch source concentration is materially larger than the 10.9%-at-8:2 figure implies, or a measured realized-share artifact from the supplier arm that was never produced. Its position-encoding caveat is recorded separately below because it outlives the idea.

THE ENCODING OBSERVATION, RULED ON AS REQUESTED. The researcher asks whether the position_1d frame mismatch is in scope. It is NOT: preparation.py:194 takes relative_position_mm, which series.py sets relative to each SERIES' own slab midpoint, while the sampler works in the fitted STUDY reference; changing that is a change to the model's input encoding, not to triplet selection, and the contract fixes the surrounding training recipe. It must not be implemented in this campaign. It IS, however, a first-class interpretation limit for two live arms and must be carried with them: median |offset| 3.21 mm, p90 11.73 mm, 35.4% of series beyond 5 mm; within multi-source pools the two suppliers' frames differ by p90 15.77 mm; across a plane's two contrasts, median 0.00 mm but p90 5.07 mm. For the reserved head, plane-coupled-depth-targets, this means co-location in the study frame does not fully reach the model as co-location in the encoded coordinate for the p90 tail of planes, so a null there is partly a null of an imperfectly delivered mechanism. Record it in the campaign documentation as an out-of-scope finding worth carrying to whatever campaign owns the input encoding.

## review-20260919T211821Z-aaae4207

Durable findings from this review, for prior-work.md.

1. RE-KEYING AN RNG SCOPE IS THE CHEAPEST WAY TO CHANGE A JOINT LAW WHILE HOLDING EVERY MARGINAL FIXED, AND IT IS FULLY VERIFIABLE STATICALLY. The entire mechanism is `scope = kind[:1] if operation in ("targets","strata") else kind` inside the rng closure. Because `rng(operation)` constructs a fresh default_rng from operation_seed(seed, study, scope, operation, epoch) per call, narrowing the scope changes WHICH stream a slot draws from without changing the construction that consumes it: each slot's own per-epoch law is distributionally identical to the incumbent's, its band partition and per-band marginals are bit-preserved (verified for count in {2,5,6} over all ten epochs), and only the correlation BETWEEN slots moves. This is the campaign's fourth distinct design pattern for a one-factor intervention, alongside scale/offset/index transformations on the vectorized quantile call, confinement to the `if training` arm, and collapsing a ternary onto its validation arm.

2. STREAM-SCOPE NARROWING IS SELECTIVE, AND THE SELECTIVITY MUST BE MEASURED RATHER THAN READ OFF THE DIFF. The constraint required "targets"/"strata" coupled and "suppliers"/"band_order" independent. Reading the tuple membership test is not evidence; drawing from both partner slots and comparing is. Confirmed here by direct comparison: partner-slot strata/targets np.array_equal at matched count, partner-slot supplier and band-order draws different. The same check should be run on any future candidate that keys some operations differently from others, because a typo in the operation tuple would silently couple a stream the design requires to stay isolable — and here that stream belongs to a family closed by a measured null, so an accidental coupling would have confounded the arm with a known negative.

3. A SHARED GENERATOR'S STATE-CONSUMPTION ORDER DETERMINES WHAT HAPPENS AT UNEQUAL SHAPES, AND THE CONSEQUENCE IS NOT WHAT THE PREFIX PROPERTY SUGGESTS. Generator.permuted(axis=1) consumes state row by row, so a count-5 and a count-7 draw at the same seed agree on rows 0-4 (confirmed again here, as at revision 97). But percentiles divide by each slot's OWN count, so identical raw stratum values map to DIFFERENT physical bands once counts diverge: the coupling degrades to independent physical alignment rather than propagating. No exception, no crash, band partition still exact. General rule: a shared raw stream is not a shared physical outcome whenever the consumers normalize by different denominators, and the campaign has now hit this at both the band-width level (bag-size pacing) and the stream level (here).

4. WHEN A BUDGET IS EQUAL WITHIN A GROUPING, THE ONLY SOURCE OF SHAPE DIVERGENCE IS CAPACITY. Verified from configs/training.yaml that base budgets are equal within every plane (max_images 18/18 axial, 15/15 coronal, 15/15 sagittal, so budgets 6/6, 5/5, 5/5). Therefore partner-slot `count` divergence can arise only from len(groups) < budget on one side, the capacity-limited minority, never from budget asymmetry. Checking the config for this kind of structural symmetry is cheap and converts an open "what if the shapes differ" question into a bounded minority case.

5. THE DEGENERATION IDIOM NOW HAS A THIRD FORM: A DEGENERATE ARGUMENT SHAPE. Prior forms were an exposed config parameter (stratification_depth 1, bag_size_pacing ()) and a structural boundary case (count == 1, single-eligible-source pools). Here it is len(kind) == 1, at which kind[:1] == kind and the candidate expression is bit-identical to the incumbent's. Thirteenth confirmation, eighth against a winner. What remains non-negotiable is that the identity is EXECUTED against the incumbent's expression rather than argued.
