## completion-20260919T214032Z-d5cca058

Durable findings from the run-level-rotated-lattice completion, for prior-work.md.

1. RESULT OF RECORD, AND THE CAMPAIGN'S FIRST REJECTION. run-level-rotated-lattice (training-only; the per-band sub-stratum draw `rng("strata", cycle).permuted(np.tile(np.arange(E),(count,1)),axis=1)[:,phase]` replaced by a single scalar `rng("strata", cycle).permuted(np.arange(E))[phase]` shared across all of a slot's bands, with the incumbent's per-band jitter `rng("targets").random(count)` retained) is REJECTED as an improvement. Reference run-level-stratified-jitter macro AUC 0.8975149, candidate 0.8933815, delta -0.0041334; paired report-group bootstrap delta mean -0.0041270, 95% interval [-0.0079360, -0.0003991] — strictly below zero — 852 groups, 2000/2000 valid draws, resampling seed 20260914, training seed 4143107527, candidate selected epoch 9 vs reference epoch 10, runtime 1581.95 s measured compute of the 1785 s ceiling, peak CUDA 20.84 GB. Evidence: experiments/run-level-rotated-lattice-20260919T204526Z-aa17feff/comparison/comparison.json and training/fold_0/{triplet_accounting,timing,selected_checkpoint}.json. The incumbent is unchanged.

2. WITHIN-BAG REGULARITY IS NOW CLOSED IN FULL, AND BOTH ITS COMPONENTS HAVE BEEN MEASURED. The expression decomposes into a coarse stratum index (spans a whole band) and a fine sub-band offset (spans 1/(count·E) of the unit interval). Sharing the FINE component across bands returned -0.0031 (inconclusive, revision 119) and was measured to be inert at the realized level. Sharing the COARSE component — the lever revision 127 deliberately reopened on exactly that inertness argument — returned -0.0041 with an interval strictly below zero. The reopening was procedurally correct (a closure is only as wide as the lever actually pulled) and it produced the decisive answer: the coarse lever is real, it moves realized regularity, and it HURTS. Working conclusion, now with a rejection behind it rather than an argument: making a single bag's targets more regular is not valuable on top of run-level stratification, and the independent per-band jitter the incumbent draws is doing useful work. No full rigid form, no partial-amplitude variant, no per-plane or per-slot subset of the sharing may be spawned.

3. THE ACCEPTED MECHANISM IS NOT "REGULARITY"; IT IS COUPLING ACROSS EPOCHS AT A FIXED PER-EPOCH MARGINAL. Ten full-budget arms now decompose as one provisional acceptance, eight inconclusives and one rejection. The acceptance (run-level-stratified-jitter, +0.0035) held each band's per-epoch marginal fixed and coordinated draws ACROSS epochs. Every attempt to coordinate draws WITHIN an epoch — across bands of a bag (both components, one rejected), across slots of a plane (cross-slot interleaving, -0.0017), across sources (supplier entitlement, -0.0029) — has returned a non-positive point estimate. State the productive axis as TEMPORAL coordination at a fixed instantaneous law, and treat any within-epoch coordination proposal as bidding against four measured negatives.

4. THE FROZEN-MODEL BAG-QUALITY EVIDENCE CLASS HAS NOW FAILED ITS LARGEST TEST AND MUST BE DEMOTED. This arm was promoted to the queue on +0.00227 (se 0.00030) of frozen-model bag quality — the largest directly measured mechanism effect the campaign ever queued, with realized min-gap p10 3.35 → 8.86 mm against the deterministic scored bag's 9.007 and a claimed 73% closure of the train/scored regularity gap — and it delivered the campaign's only strictly negative interval. That evidence class (fold-0 VALIDATION cohort, retired sigma-40 checkpoints trained under the un-stratified baseline sampler) has now predicted the sign of 1 of 7 arms. RULE: it may generate hypotheses but must never promote an idea above its structural merits, and where it conflicts with an invariance argument the invariance argument wins. Corollary worth keeping: a measured train/scored MISMATCH is not by itself a reason to remove the mismatch — this arm removed one and lost, the supplier arm removed another and lost, and cross-slot interleaving moved a third and lost.

5. A CANDIDATE CAN BE STRUCTURALLY IMPECCABLE AND STILL BE REJECTED, WHICH IS THE POINT OF RUNNING IT. Every invariant checked at review held in the run: band partition and per-band marginals preserved, run-level sub-stratum coverage preserved (each band still visits all ten sub-strata), count==1 degeneration bit-identical to the winner's expression, validation identity bit-for-bit, counts exact, cost strictly lower, memory unchanged. Static verification establishes that an arm measures ONE factor cleanly; it says nothing about the sign. This is the cleanest available demonstration that the campaign's review standard is a confound-elimination standard, not a quality predictor, and it is the reason a rejection here is informative rather than wasted.

6. COUNT INVARIANCE HOLDS FOR EIGHT OF EIGHT MEASURED CANDIDATES, INCLUDING FOUR THAT CHANGED THE PER-EPOCH JOINT LAW. Exactly 93,846 triplets and 3,503 visits in every one of the ten training epochs (938,460 / 35,030), 24,188 per validation pass, at zero tolerance. The requirement is unchanged and cheap: any candidate that MOVES targets inherits neither the revision-61 empirical invariance nor the revision-121 cardinality argument (which covers only interventions strictly downstream of `targets`) and must establish counts in its own replay. Pools average capacity 36.3 against budgets of 5-6, so linear_sum_assignment remains far from Hall-tight.

7. FOURTH CONSECUTIVE BIT-FOR-BIT VALIDATION-IDENTITY CONFIRMATION (92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 on all ten rows, ten distinct training hashes). The design that produces it — confine the change to the `if training` arm and leave the `else` literal `(np.arange(count)+0.5)/count` untouched, or collapse a ternary onto its validation arm — converts validation seed-independence from an argued property into a machine-checked one and immunizes the arm against the ~0.0006 matched-condition effect. preprocessing_identity still differs from any SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit; never conflate the two.

No artifact records realized middle depths, the adjacent min-gap against the incumbent's p10 3.345 / median 7.203 mm, or distinct realized middles per pool. Because geometry_groups plus linear_sum_assignment snap targets onto discrete candidate slices, how much of the intended target-level regularity actually reached the bags is unknown, so what is rejected is the TARGET-LEVEL shared-coarse-stratum intervention. The interval is strictly negative regardless, so the decision is unaffected — but a rejection with an unmeasured realized effect cannot tell us whether a weaker dose would behave differently, and it must not be used to argue one would. ## review-20260919T214809Z-7a639868

Durable findings from this review, for prior-work.md.

1. A LOW-DISCREPANCY VISITATION ORDER PRESERVES AN ACCEPTED RUN-LEVEL COVERAGE PROPERTY EXACTLY, AND THE CHECK IS A PERMUTATION TEST PER BAND. Replacing a per-band uniform random permutation of sub-strata with (r_i + sigma(e)) mod E, r_i an independent uniform integer per band and sigma a fixed permutation of positions, leaves every band's ten-epoch sequence a permutation of 0..E-1 — verified by execution for count in {5,6} in BOTH candidate and incumbent. This is the fourth part of the composition check (revision 159 finding 4) applied positively: the accepted winner's PROPERTY, not merely its code, is what must survive, and here it survives bit-exactly while the temporal order changes. Measured mechanism delta at the stratum level: consecutive-epoch adjacent-stratum rate (|d| = 1 mod E) falls from 0.204 (count 6) / 0.244 (count 5) to exactly 0.000, because stride 3 cannot land adjacent; same-stratum rate is 0.000 in both.

2. "A FIXED SHIFT OF AN INDEPENDENT UNIFORM IS INDEPENDENT UNIFORM" IS THE STRONGEST FORM OF PER-EPOCH LAW PRESERVATION THE CAMPAIGN HAS SEEN, AND IT IS BOTH ANALYTIC AND MEASURABLE. Unlike every prior coupling arm, this construction preserves the across-band per-epoch joint law exactly rather than approximately: confirmed over 20,000 distinct studies at count 6 with pooled marginal chi-square p = 0.702 and maximum absolute off-diagonal cross-band correlation 0.0120, BELOW the incumbent's own recorded 0.0181. Where an invariance can be argued analytically, the execution check should be run anyway and reported as agreement with the analysis, not as the analysis's substitute.

3. A SHARED VISITATION ORDER FREEZES CROSS-BAND RELATIVE OFFSETS FOR THE WHOLE RUN, AND THAT IS A SECOND FACTOR THAT MUST BE DISCLOSED EVEN WHEN THE PER-EPOCH LAW IS UNTOUCHED. Measured: with count 6 the band-minus-band-0 offset vector is identical at all ten epochs ([0,8,6,8,7,2]), whereas independent per-band permutations re-randomize it each epoch. So the shared-order form changes both the per-band order (random to low-discrepancy) and the temporal rigidity across bands. It is NOT the factor rejected in run-level-rotated-lattice, which coupled band LEVELS within an epoch; this couples band INCREMENTS across epochs at an exactly unchanged instantaneous law. But it makes a negative result ambiguous between "timing does not matter" and "rigid translation hurts", and that ambiguity is the price paid for one-factor minimality when the per-band direction bit was declined.

4. THE DEGENERATION IDIOM SURVIVES A HELPER FUNCTION, AND DEGENERATE ARITHMETIC MUST BE CHECKED IN THE HELPER ITSELF. stratum_visit_order(1) computes round(1/3) = 0 and math.gcd(0, 1) = 1, returning stride 0 and order [0]; the candidate array is then bit-identical to the incumbent's at E = 1 for count in {1,5,6} across all ten epochs. When a mechanism is factored into a helper, the degenerate parameter must be pushed through the helper's own arithmetic (integer rounding, gcd, modulus) rather than only through the call site. Fourteenth confirmation of the idiom, ninth against a winner.

5. A CONFINED-TO-`if training` CHANGE PLUS AN UNREACHABLE HELPER IS THE STRONGEST VALIDATION-INDEPENDENCE ARGUMENT AVAILABLE, AND UNREACHABILITY SHOULD BE VERIFIED AT EVERY CALL SITE. Here both the rng closure and the new helper are invoked only inside the training arm, and the remaining rng call sites are training-gated ternaries, so the deterministic branch is the untouched literal `(np.arange(count) + 0.5) / count`. Sixth candidate to predict the sealed 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 in advance after four consecutive bit-for-bit confirmations. preprocessing_identity still differs from any SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit; never conflate the two.

6. AN INLINE PER-CALL COMPUTATION IS NOT A PRECOMPUTATION ARTIFACT, AND THE DISTINCTION MATTERS FOR THE RESOURCE CEILING. The visit order is recomputed inside select_triplets on every call rather than cached outside the measured process, so it carries no preparation-boundary question at all; preparation_seconds 0.4282 is the campaign's largest recorded but remains negligible against the ~200 s of headroom. Reconfirmed for the eleventh consecutive arm: sampler-side per-call deltas of a few microseconds are invisible against the +24 to +30 s every run lands over the control, and only per-element Python loops over candidates (the measured +24.6 s case) are large enough to matter.

7. COUNT ENTITLEMENTS REMAIN NON-TRANSFERABLE. Even with the per-epoch across-band law exactly preserved, this candidate MOVES targets and therefore inherits neither the revision-61 empirical invariance nor the revision-121 cardinality argument (which covers only interventions strictly downstream of `targets`). Counts must be established in its own replay. Eight of eight measured candidates have realized zero shortfall against mean pool capacity 36.3 and budgets of 5-6, but that is context, not evidence.

8. A DEVELOPER REPORT THAT REFUSES TO CONFLATE TWO SIMILAR STATISTICS IS MORE USEFUL THAN ONE THAT ASSERTS SUCCESS. The raw stratum-index consecutive-repeat statistic (trivially 0% for both arms, since both guarantee a per-cycle permutation) is NOT the geometry-group-level statistic that motivates the idea (15.66% -> 4.75%), and the developer said so explicitly rather than presenting the reachable check as the motivating one. Recorded as precedent alongside revision 89 finding 7: reports that bound their own claims improve review quality materially.

9. THE REALIZED-EXPOSURE READ-BACK REACHES ELEVEN CONSECUTIVE UNRECORDED OCCURRENCES, BUT THIS ARM SPLITS IT USEFULLY. Half of its required read-back (distinct realized middles per pool over the run, which must be UNCHANGED) is a quantity the replay already computes; the discriminating half (per-band distinct-realized-groups after k epochs, at the 2- and 3-epoch prefixes, plus the consecutive-epoch same-GROUP rate) is not. The design implication from revision 133 sharpens: where only part of a mechanism's evidence is free, the bespoke part must be produced at the replay step or the completion records the mechanism claim as unverified — a null on an unmeasured realized effect cannot bound a weaker dose and must not be used to argue one.

10. accounting.py:78 derives selection_sha256 from the sorted (series_type_index, selected paths) rows and never reads metadata; TripletLedger.observe counts from batch["slice_mask"].sum(dim=1) with dynamic padding, so the gate measures real selections and a too-large bag fails by OOM abort rather than by miscount; realized statistics from the replay, never requested/target quantities, are the evidence for any mechanism-level claim; express sampler policy as a scale, offset or index transformation on the existing vectorized quantile call; stratification_depth is pinned at 10 to the frozen ten-epoch schedule and must not be swept; all cache-derived motivating statistics come from the fold-0 VALIDATION cohort under retired sigma-40 checkpoints and have never been re-measured on the training cohort or against models trained under the current incumbent — that evidence class has predicted the sign of 1 of 7 arms and produced the campaign's only strictly negative interval, so it must never promote an idea above its structural merits; the productive axis remains TEMPORAL coordination at a fixed instantaneous law, with within-epoch coordination across bands, slots and sources all measured non-positive; within-bag regularity is closed in both components, along with the draw-shape family, across-epoch budget pacing, training-supplier-allocation, cross-slot DE-correlation and the revision-155 falsifications; the sole acceptance remains provisional at a +0.00025 lower bound with a fresh-seed confirmation reserved for a second acceptance stacking on it; and the comparison tool's limitations (one training seed, one development fold, intervals excluding training-seed and checkpoint-selection variability, allowed config and identity differences moving together) remain the honest frame for every decision here.

1. SCOPE AND SNAPSHOT. `diff -rq source workspace` is EMPTY, so the immutable snapshot is complete and byte-equal to the reviewed workspace. `diff -rq` against parent_source (run-level-stratified-jitter/source, the incumbent) shows exactly ONE differing file: src/knee_abnormality/data/preprocessing/sampling.py. configs/training.yaml is BYTE-IDENTICAL to the parent (diff -q confirms), and the resolved TripletSamplingConfig exposes exactly ['stratification_depth'] = 10 with max_epochs 10 and the placeholder seed 2583306572 the supervisor must replace, so no new config key exists and allowed config differences must reduce exactly to name / paths.output_root / seed. 3. THE DIRECTED CONSTRAINT IS MET EXACTLY. - THE MECHANISM'S OWN STATISTIC MOVES AS CLAIMED, at the stratum level: consecutive-epoch adjacent-stratum rate (|d| = 1 mod 10) is 0.000 for the candidate against 0.204 (count 6) and 0.244 (count 5) for the incumbent, and the same-stratum rate is 0.000 for both. Stride 3 cannot land adjacent, as designed.
- DEGENERATION AT stratification_depth = 1 IS BIT-IDENTICAL to the incumbent's expression for count in {1,5,6} across all ten epochs (np.array_equal). Note the helper is well defined there: round(1/3) = 0 and math.gcd(0,1) = 1, so stride 0 and visit_order [0]. Fourteenth confirmation of the campaign idiom, ninth against a winner.

5. VALIDATION INDEPENDENCE FROM THE TRAINING SEED. The change is wholly inside the `if training:` arm; the `else` literal `(np.arange(count) + 0.5) / count` is untouched, and both the `rng` closure and `stratum_visit_order` are unreachable when training_seed is None (the only other rng call sites are `supplier_rng = ... if training else None` and the training-gated `order` ternary). Deterministic selection is therefore bit-identical to the incumbent's, and 92f1341ce45b14ee27882fd20892bd416b7945d85ddcd297055441fbc1784059 is again the hard, falsifiable launch check — the sixth candidate to predict this invariance in advance, after four consecutive bit-for-bit confirmations. Standing distinction, not to be conflated: preprocessing_identity WILL differ (SAMPLING_IDENTITY edit) while the validation SELECTION hash must be unchanged.

6. RELATION TO THE REJECTION, RE-CONFIRMED BY MEASUREMENT RATHER THAN RESTATED. run-level-rotated-lattice was rejected for coupling band LEVELS within an epoch (one scalar shared across a slot's bands). This candidate leaves the within-epoch across-band law exactly independent uniform (max correlation 0.0120, below the incumbent's own 0.0181), so it does not touch the rejected factor. The revision-165 ruling stands and is now backed by execution on the delivered diff.

7. COUNT ACCOUNTING. `count = min(budget, len(groups))` is untouched, percentiles retain length count, strata stay in [0, E-1], and targets feed only target_depths. But the candidate MOVES targets, so it inherits neither the revision-61 empirical invariance nor the revision-121 cardinality argument (which covers only interventions strictly downstream of `targets`, confirmed by measurement on the supplier arm). Counts must be established in its own replay — exactly as the developer states rather than glosses. Supporting expectation only: eight of eight measured candidates realized zero shortfall, and pools average capacity 36.3 against budgets of 5-6.

8. LEAKAGE, COVERAGE, EXPLOITATION, PREPROCESSING, COST. No label, fold, cohort, grouping, scoring or checkpoint-selection code touched; no validation-derived feedback; no persistent precomputation — `stratum_visit_order` runs inline per call inside the measured training process, not as a cached artifact, and it is a length-E integer expression, not a per-element Python loop over candidates, so the standing vectorized-path rule is satisfied. No extra decode pass. Band masses, supports and per-band marginals are unchanged, so no depth region loses support and peripheral bands are drawn exactly as often as today; the full-run realized set is identical by construction. Bag sizes are unchanged, so the incumbent's 20.83 GB peak (±~0.5 GB run-to-run, 24 GB device) applies unmodified and there is no memory concern. preparation_seconds 0.4282 — the largest the campaign has recorded but still negligible against the ~200 s of headroom below the 1,785 s ceiling; the developer's ~10.8 us against ~9.5 us per-call figure is the same order as every prior sampler-side delta, which ten arms have shown to be invisible against the +24 to +30 s every run lands regardless.

THREE INTERPRETATION LIMITS RECORDED RATHER THAN REPAIRED (none is a contract breach; the one allowed repair is not spent).
(i) THE SHARED ORDER FREEZES THE CROSS-BAND RELATIVE OFFSETS FOR THE WHOLE RUN, and I measured this rather than inferring it: with count 6, the band-minus-band-0 offset vector is [0,8,6,8,7,2] at every one of the ten epochs, identical across the run, whereas the incumbent's independent per-band permutations re-randomize it each epoch. So the candidate changes TWO things relative to the incumbent — each band's order becomes low-discrepancy instead of random, AND all bands advance in lockstep. The per-epoch joint law is untouched either way, so this is not the rejected factor; but it does mean a negative result would be ambiguous between "exposure timing does not matter" and "the rigid temporal translation hurts". This is the exact cost the idea's open_choices (i) disclosed and that revision 153 accepted deliberately in exchange for one-factor minimality; the per-band direction bit must still NOT be spawned as arm two.
(ii) NO BAG-QUALITY PRIOR IN EITHER DIRECTION. The frozen-model token cache scores single bags and this changes nothing about any single bag, so — uniquely among accepted arms — there is no cheap prior, and the effect could be zero. Recorded at revision 153 and strengthened by revision 165: the evidence class that would have supplied one has now predicted the sign of 1 of 7 arms and produced the campaign's only strictly negative interval. The motivating quantities (+5.3% LR-weighted exposure diversity, 3.3x fewer consecutive same-group repeats) have no established conversion to macro AUC and may fall below the ~0.0037 interval half-width. The LR-mass weighting also assumes gradient contribution scales with learning rate, a standard but untested proxy.
(iii) THE MOTIVATING EMULATION IS ON THE FOLD-0 VALIDATION COHORT under a nearest-depth approximation; the training cohort's pool geometry has never been measured. The developer correctly reports that the geometry-group-level read-back (consecutive same-GROUP rate 15.66% -> 4.75%, LR-weighted distinct-after-k 2.9219 -> 3.0765) could not be reproduced without that cache, and correctly refuses to conflate it with the raw stratum-index statistic I verified in item 4 — those are different quantities and the group-level one remains unverified pending the replay. That refusal is the right conduct and is recorded as such.

LAUNCH CONDITIONS the supervisor must honor (no limit relaxed, nothing inherited from any winner):
1. One-time full-schedule accounting.calibrate() CPU replay BEFORE any GPU run: training_triplets exactly 938460, training_visits exactly 35030, validation_triplets exactly 24188 per pass, zero tolerance. MANDATORY: the candidate moves targets and inherits no count entitlement.

Any deviation on the validation hash is a hard failure of the stated mechanism, not a new recordable identity.
3. ENFORCE AT THE REPLAY STEP, not by restating at approval: read back REALIZED middle depths and record (a) distinct realized middles per pool over the run, which must be UNCHANGED against the incumbent (~27.0 emulated) — this is the half of the read-back the replay already computes; (b) the per-band distinct-realized-geometry-groups AFTER k EPOCHS curve, with the 2- and 3-epoch prefixes as the discriminating statistic (emulated 1.953 / 2.894 against the incumbent's 1.843 / 2.560); and (c) the consecutive-epoch same-geometry-group rate against the incumbent's 15.66%. Per limit (iii) this is the arm's ONLY direct mechanism evidence at the level that motivates it; the read-back has now gone unrecorded ELEVEN consecutive completions and has already cost one attribution outright. If the artifact is absent, the completion must record the mechanism claim as unverified.
4. Comparison requires --allowed-input-identity-differences preprocessing_identity; changed_source_archive_members must be exactly ["src/knee_abnormality/data/preprocessing/sampling.py"]. Allowed config differences must reduce exactly to name / paths.output_root / seed — stratification_depth will NOT appear, the incumbent being the parent.
5. Draw and record a fresh seed at launch, replacing 2583306572.
6. Reference incumbent is run-level-stratified-jitter, source .../run-level-stratified-jitter-20260919T183826Z-f211c7ce/source, run .../training, predictions sha256 cd78146e9263cdb9060e7c49cf472211c0bd5f41cf20e2d94367b2a9e2533e4c, macro AUC 0.8975149 — NOT the historical control — under the sealed 2,000-draw paired report-group bootstrap at resampling seed 20260914, 95% interval. Its acceptance remains provisional at a +0.00025 lower bound.
7. No memory concern: bag sizes unchanged.
8. stratification_depth stays 10 and must not be swept; no per-band direction bit, no alternative stride, and no greedy circular-dispersion variant may be spawned as arm two.
ORDERING: plane-coupled-depth-targets is training now and re-keys the same "strata" stream on (kind[0],). If it is ACCEPTED, this candidate must be rebased before launch — the shared per-plane stream becomes the expression the visit order rotates, the accepted component must be preserved bit-for-bit, and the revision-89/141/159 composition check applies including which PROPERTY of the winner survives, not only which code. If it is not accepted, the approved diff stands as-is. This is the only remaining rebase trigger.

RANKING of the live queue (2 entries; head reserved and first by the reservation rule; no new evidence in this event bears on the tail).
1. prefix-balanced-stratum-order — RESERVED HEAD, approved here, status prepared. It is the only live idea in the one mechanism class that has ever moved this campaign's metric (temporal coordination at a fixed instantaneous law), and it holds more invariants fixed by construction than any candidate the campaign has run: per-epoch across-band joint law exactly the incumbent's, full-run realized sub-stratum set per band identical, band partition, supports and marginals bit-preserved, validation identity predicted exactly, cost neutral, degeneration bit-exact at E = 1 — all five verified by execution above. That makes both outcomes cheap to interpret, subject to limit (i). Its weakness is magnitude and the absence of any prior, per limits (ii) and (iii), not validity.
2. physical-span-standardized-triplets — unchanged at last, and now the only tail entry. The only idea changing what a triplet IS, narrowest reach (~6% of pools below 1 mm median spacing), and the only one needing the preparation.py scope widening (granted, limited to preparation.py:125-130 and its immediate callers; both preconditions unchanged: inspect the p1 0.03 mm pools before assuming they are recoverable, and confirm unique-decode counts in the replay). Note for its eventual development: revision 155 measured that no realized-versus-intended depth-density defect exists and that no free per-candidate quality signal beyond depth exists, so its case rests entirely on through-plane channel content, not on placement.

SURPLUS RETAINED, NOT QUEUED: run-level-balanced-supplier-cycling (requeueable only on the revision-153 conditions) and the bimodal / compartment-weighted sagittal draw. I am not queueing either to fill depth.

CLOSED DIRECTIONS: unchanged from revision 165 in full, including within-bag regularity closed in BOTH its coarse (rejected) and fine (inconclusive) components with no full rigid form, partial-amplitude or per-plane/per-slot subset; training-supplier-allocation; across-epoch budget pacing at the amplitudes the ceiling permits; cross-slot DE-correlation (the in-flight plane-coupling arm is the disclosed opposite-direction exception); and the five directions falsified by measurement at revision 155 plus the even-E orthomorphism obstruction. Both comparisons are inconclusive under the unchanged campaign rule. This records insufficient repeatable benefit to retain the implementation, not statistically established harm. Do not retry the same implementation or draw more seeds to recover a win; a revisit requires new evidence or a materially changed mechanism under a new idea ID.
